Furthermore, the ablation study results demonstrate the usefulness of the components of MIMO-ESP, emphasizing the potential of the proposed approaches.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物病害导致农作物减产，传统人工检测方法耗时耗力，亟需自动化解决方案；而现有公开数据集不足以支持构建高效病害识别模型，限制了机器学习特别是CNN在该领域的应用效果。

Method: 系统调研并评估现有公开植物病害数据集与模型性能，构建新的高质量数据集，并结合数据增强研究设计基于DenseNet201的新基础模型；通过迁移学习在多个数据集上进行实验验证其有效性。

Result: 提出的新DenseNet201基础模型在自建数据集上优于基线模型，并在迁移学习任务中表现出更快的收敛速度、更高的稳定性与鲁棒性，且能在较少数据下实现更好性能。

Conclusion: 构建合适的数据集与专用基础模型能显著提升植物叶片病害分类的自动化水平，所提出的模型为该领域提供了更高效的迁移学习解决方案，有助于推动精准农业的发展。

Abstract: Plants, crops and their yields are essential to our very existence, but diseases and pests cause large losses every year. As such it is vital to ensure that diseases can be spotted early and treated accordingly and stopping the spread while still possible. Manual and traditional methods require personal to walk through the field and check for symptoms 'by hand'. This is very laborious and very time consuming, so ML methods have been applied as a result and they have garnered promising results. CNN models are especially efficient as they can automatically extract features from images without any manual feature construction before then feeding the features to a classifier. Datasets are largely influential to the final performance of the model. Despite the importance that datasets pose to the field, there still seems to be somewhat of a discrepancy between what is publicly available for use and what would be required to sufficiently train fully capable models. To overcome these shortcomings, as part of this thesis open datasets for the field of plant leaf disease classification have been identified as well as models that can be trained on them and extensive benchmarks have been carried out to identify their suitability. Then a new dataset was constructed based on those findings as well as on the findings of a augmentation applicability study, which will be used to train a new Base Model based on the DenseNet201 architecture, which managed to outperform the baseline model on said new dataset as well as outperforming it on plant leaf disease classification domain specific Transfer-Learning experiments on another new dataset. This new model manages to train models through Transfer-Learning (TL) faster, more robust, more stable, and with less data than general model would, overcoming a large number of issues that the field still suffers from.

### [39] [Chain of Evidence: Pixel-Level Visual Attribution for Iterative Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01284)
*Peiyang Liu,Ziqiang Cui,Xi Wang,Di Liang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“证据链（Chain of Evidence, CoE）”的视觉归因框架，用于增强迭代检索增强生成（iRAG），通过直接在文档截图上利用视觉语言模型进行推理，解决了现有系统中文本级引用粗粒度和视觉语义丢失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的iRAG系统主要依赖于解析后的文本，导致两个关键瓶颈：一是引用粒度过粗，用户难以定位具体证据；二是将富含视觉信息的文档转为文本时丢失了布局和空间逻辑，影响推理效果。

Method: 提出CoE框架，该框架不依赖特定检索器，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）直接对检索到的文档截图进行推理，输出精确的边界框以可视化整个推理链，无需格式特定的解析过程。

Result: 在两个基准数据集Wiki-CoE和SlideVQA上进行了实验，结果表明微调后的Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型在需要理解视觉布局的任务中显著优于基于文本的基线方法。

Conclusion: CoE提供了一种通用、可解释的像素级iRAG解决方案，能够在多跳复杂问答中有效利用视觉丰富的文档，提升系统的可追溯性和推理能力。

Abstract: Iterative Retrieval-Augmented Generation (iRAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for answering complex multi-hop questions by progressively retrieving and reasoning over external documents. However, current systems predominantly operate on parsed text, which creates two critical bottlenecks: (1) \textit{Coarse-grained attribution}, where users are burdened with manually locating evidence within lengthy documents based on vague text-level citations; and (2) \textit{Visual semantic loss}, where the conversion of visually rich documents (e.g., slides, PDFs with charts) into text discards spatial logic and layout cues essential for reasoning. To bridge this gap, we present \textbf{Chain of Evidence (CoE)}, a retriever-agnostic visual attribution framework that leverages Vision-Language Models to reason directly over screenshots of retrieved document candidates. CoE eliminates format-specific parsing and outputs precise bounding boxes, visualizing the complete reasoning chain within the retrieved candidate set. We evaluate CoE on two distinct benchmarks: \textbf{Wiki-CoE}, a large-scale dataset of structured web pages derived from 2WikiMultiHopQA, and \textbf{SlideVQA}, a challenging dataset of presentation slides featuring complex diagrams and free-form layouts. Experiments demonstrate that fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct achieves robust performance, significantly outperforming text-based baselines in scenarios requiring visual layout understanding, while establishing a retriever-agnostic solution for pixel-level interpretable iRAG. Our code is available at https://github.com/PeiYangLiu/CoE.git.

</details>


### [40] [SIFT-VTON: Geometric Correspondence Supervision on Cross-Attention for Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01296)
*Kosuke Takemoto,Takafumi Koshinaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SIFT-VTON的新方法，通过结合SIFT关键点匹配为基于扩散的虚拟试穿提供显式几何引导，提升了服装细节（如文字和图案）的保留效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的虚拟试穿方法依赖于隐式学习空间对应关系，难以保持服装上的精细细节，如文字和图案。因此需要一种能够显式指导空间对齐的方法来提升生成质量。

Method: 提出SIFT-VTON，利用SIFT关键点匹配提取服装与人体之间的几何对应关系；通过领域特定的过滤策略筛选匹配点，并将其转化为空间概率分布，用于监督扩散模型中的交叉注意力层训练过程。

Result: 在VITON-HD数据集上的实验表明，该方法在非配对指标上显著优于现有方法，同时在配对重建指标上保持竞争力；定性结果显示文字清晰度和图案对齐效果更好；注意力可视化证实了模型能更精准聚焦于相关细节区域。

Conclusion: 本工作证明了经典几何匹配方法（如SIFT）可有效增强现代扩散模型在条件生成任务中的性能，特别是在需要精确空间对齐的虚拟试穿场景中具有潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion-based virtual try-on methods achieve photorealistic synthesis through cross-attention mechanisms that transfer garment features to target body regions. However, these approaches rely on implicit learning of spatial correspondences, struggling to preserve fine details such as text and illustrations. We propose a novel approach, which we call SIFT-VTON, that utilizes SIFT keypoint matching to provide explicit geometric guidance for diffusion-based virtual try-on. Our method applies domain-specific filtering to SIFT keypoint matches between garment and person images, then converts these correspondences into spatial probability distributions that supervise cross-attention layers during training. This explicit supervision guides the model to learn precise spatial alignment, concentrating attention on geometrically consistent garment regions. Experiments on the VITON-HD dataset demonstrate significant improvements on unpaired metrics while maintaining competitive paired reconstruction metrics. Qualitative comparisons show superior preservation of text clarity and pattern alignment. Attention visualizations confirm that our method produces sharply focused attention on relevant garment details. This work demonstrates that classical geometric correspondence methods can effectively enhance modern diffusion models for conditional synthesis tasks. The source code will be available at https://github.com/takesukeDS/SIFT-VTON.

</details>


### [41] [CUE: Concept-Aware Multi-Label Expansion to Mitigate Concept Confusion in Long-Tailed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01309)
*Ruichi Zhang,Chikai Shang,Jiacheng Yang,Mengke Li,Yang Zhou,Junlong Gao,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CUE（Concept-aware mUlti-label Expansion）的方法，用于解决长尾分布下的单标签监督导致的概念混淆问题。通过结合零样本CLIP提取的实例级视觉线索和大语言模型生成的类级语义线索，引入多标签概念信号以恢复被破坏的类别间关系，并采用二元Logit调整损失进行优化，在多个长尾基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长尾学习方法主要关注缓解数据分布偏倚，但忽视了由长尾分布引起的概念混淆问题，特别是单标签监督下的互斥性会抑制相关类别间的特征共享，损害类别间的判别能力。

Method: 提出CUE方法，构建包含实例级视觉线索（来自CLIP）和类级语义线索（来自LLM）的概念集，通过加权的二元Logit-Adjustment（BLA）辅助损失分别融合两种线索，并与基础的Logit-Adjustment（LA）损失联合优化。

Result: 在多个长尾基准测试上，CUE实现了均衡且强劲的性能，超过了近期的最先进方法。

Conclusion: CUE有效缓解了长尾分布下因单标签监督导致的概念混淆问题，通过引入多标签概念信号增强了类别间的语义关联与判别能力，为长尾学习提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Long-tailed distributions are common in real-world recognition tasks, where a few head classes have many samples while most tail classes have very few. Recently, fine-tuning foundation models for long-tailed learning has gained attention due to their excellent performance. However, most existing methods focus solely on mitigating long-tailed distribution bias while overlooking concept confusion caused by the long-tailed distribution. In this paper, we study this problem and attribute it to the mutual exclusivity of single-label supervision under long-tailed distributions, which suppresses feature sharing among related classes and amplifies the dominance of head classes, leading to disrupted inter-class discriminability. To address this, we propose CUE, Concept-aware mUlti-label Expansion, which introduces multi-label concept signals to preserve disrupted inter-class relationships. Specifically, CUE constructs concept sets by (i) extracting instance-level visual cues from zero-shot CLIP and (ii) generating class-level semantic cues with LLM; the two cues are incorporated via separately weighted Binary Logit-Adjustment (BLA) auxiliary losses and jointly optimized with the baseline Logit-Adjustment (LA) loss. Experiments on several long-tailed benchmarks, CUE achieves balanced and strong performance, surpassing recent state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at: https://github.com/zhangruichi/CUE.

</details>


### [42] [Beyond Perceptual Shortcuts: Causal-Inspired Debiasing Optimization for Generalizable Video Reasoning in Lightweight MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01324)
*Jingze Wu,Quan Zhang,Hongfei Suo,Zeqiang Cai,Hongbo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VideoThinker的因果启发式框架，通过两阶段去偏过程提升轻量级多模态语言模型在视频推理任务中的推理能力，有效缓解了强化学习微调中的感知偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现现有强化学习方法在轻量级模型上容易导致感知偏差，使其依赖数据中的捷径而非真正推理，限制了模型泛化能力。

Method: 提出VideoThinker框架，包含两个阶段：1）偏见感知训练阶段构建一个专门捕捉捷径行为的‘偏见模型’；2）因果去偏策略优化（CDPO）算法通过排斥该偏见模型的逻辑并吸引正确解来微调主模型。

Result: 所提出的VideoThinker-R1在相同规模下无需监督微调且仅用1/10的RL数据，就在多个基准上超越VideoRFT-3B（平均+3.2%，VideoMME +7%）；跨规模比较中也优于更大的Video-UTR-7B（MVBench +2.1%，TempCompass +3.8%）。

Conclusion: VideoThinker通过因果分析指导的去偏机制，显著提升了轻量级模型在视频理解中的真实推理能力，为边缘部署提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Although reinforcement learning (RL) has significantly advanced reasoning capabilities in large multimodal language models (MLLMs), its efficacy remains limited for lightweight models essential for edge deployments.To address this issue, we leverage causal analysis and experiment to reveal the underlying phenomenon of perceptual bias, demonstrating that RL-based fine-tuning compels lightweight models to preferentially adopt perceptual shortcuts induced by data biases, rather than developing genuine reasoning abilities.Motivated by this insight, we propose VideoThinker, a causal-inspired framework that cultivates robust reasoning in lightweight models through a two-stage debiasing process. First, the Bias Aware Training stage forges a dedicated "bias model" to embody these shortcut behaviors. Then, the Causal Debiasing Policy Optimization (CDPO) algorithm fine-tunes the primary model, employing an innovative repulsive objective to actively push it away from the bias model's flawed logic while simultaneously pulling it toward correct, generalizable solutions.Our model, VideoThinker-R1, establishes a new state-of-the-art in video reasoning efficiency. For same-scale comparison, requiring no Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and using only 1 of the training data for RL, it surpasses VideoRFT-3B with a 3.2% average gain on widely-used benchmarks and a 7% lead on VideoMME. For cross-scale comparison, it outperforms the larger Video-UTR-7B model on multiple benchmarks, including a 2.1% gain on MVBench and a 3.8% gain on TempCompass. Code is available at https://github.com/falonss703/VideoThinker.

</details>


### [43] [Rethinking Model Selection in VLM Through the Lens of Gromov-Wasserstein Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01325)
*Muyang Li,Yucheng Liu,Jianbo Ma,Elliot Osborne,Bo Han,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉-语言模型（VLM）中视觉编码器的选择问题，提出跨模态结构相似性（通过Gromov-Wasserstein距离衡量）是影响VLM性能的关键因素，并通过理论和实验证明该指标能有效预测VLM表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择视觉编码器的方法（如参数量或零样本准确率）与VLM性能相关性弱，缺乏对何种视觉编码器更适合VLM对齐的系统理解。

Method: 在19个预训练视觉编码器上进行综合实验，使用Gromov-Wasserstein距离衡量跨模态结构相似性，并结合60多次完整VLM训练进行实证分析。

Result: 发现传统指标与VLM性能仅弱至中度相关，而Gromov-Wasserstein距离作为推理阶段即可获得的指标，与最终VLM性能有更强的相关性，显著优于其他选择策略。

Conclusion: 跨模态结构相似性是选择适合VLM的视觉编码器的关键因素，提出的基于Gromov-Wasserstein距离的度量可高效且有效地预测VLM性能，避免昂贵的全量训练。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enhanced traditional LLMs with visual capabilities through the integration of vision encoders. While recent works have explored various combinations of vision encoders and LLMs, there still lacks a principled understanding of what makes a vision encoder suitable for VLM alignment. In this paper, we systematically investigate this question via comprehensive experiments on a curated collection of 19 pre-trained vision encoders from diverse sources. We first demonstrate that common practices, such as choosing encoders with the largest size or highest zero-shot accuracy, consistently fail to identify optimal models. In fact, these metrics show only weak to moderate correlation with VLM performance. This intriguing finding begs a fundamental question: What factors of vision-encoders matter in VLM? Through comprehensive analysis, we identify that the structural similarity across modalities plays a crucial but previously overlooked role in vision-encoder selection, which we measure using the Gromov-Wasserstein distance as a proxy. From a theoretical perspective, we show that the learnability of cross-modality mapping can be provably associated with the Gromov-Wasserstein distance. Empirical verification on 60+ full VLM training runs shows that our proposed inference-only metric performs significantly better than alternative model selection strategies and exhibits a much stronger correlation with final VLM performance, thereby enabling efficient and effective prediction of VLM performance before full training.

</details>


### [44] [Colinearity Decay: Training Quantization-Friendly ViTs with Outlier Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01330)
*Jin Tong,Guang Liang,Peilin Sun,Jianxin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Colinearity-Decay（CD）的结构正则化方法，用于改善视觉Transformer在低比特量化部署中的表现，通过控制导致激活异常的结构放大效应，而非简单抑制异常值。该方法在不改变架构和任务损失的前提下，有效缓解极端激活，提升量化精度，同时保持甚至改善全精度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化虽有助于高效部署视觉Transformer，但激活值中的离群点使其难以实现完全量化。现有方法在训练后量化或训练中抑制大激活值，但过度压制会影响全精度与量化精度之间的平衡。因此，需要一种更优的训练目标来控制导致离群点有害的结构放大效应。

Method: 提出Colinearity-Decay（CD）作为Transformer模块中有序矩阵对的结构正则项，惩罚有害的跨矩阵对齐，从而减轻极端激活。CD以解耦更新方式应用，不侵入原有训练流程，引入极小训练开销。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K预训练、COCO检测和下游微调任务中，CD在多个量化流程中持续提升量化精度，同时保持甚至提升全精度模型性能。

Conclusion: 结构正则化能有效为视觉Transformer的低比特部署做好准备，且无需任何推理时开销。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization is a practical route for efficiently deploying vision Transformers, yet activation outliers complicate fully quantized deployment. Existing methods either handle quantization post-training or suppress large activations during training; however, aggressively restricting outliers in vision models can lead to a poorer trade-off between full-precision and quantized accuracy. We argue that rather than simply suppressing outliers, the training objective should control the structural amplification that makes them harmful. To this end, we introduce Colinearity-Decay (CD), a structural regularizer for ordered matrix pairs within Transformer blocks. CD penalizes detrimental cross-matrix alignment and mitigates extreme activations without altering the architecture or task loss. Applied as a decoupled update, CD is non-invasive and introduces minimal training overhead. Across ImageNet-1K pre-training, COCO detection, and downstream fine-tuning, CD consistently boosts quantized accuracy across multiple pipelines while preserving, or even improving, full-precision performance. Ultimately, our results demonstrate that structural regularization effectively prepares vision Transformers for low-bit deployment with zero inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [45] [Zero-Shot Interpretable Image Steganalysis for Invertible Image Hiding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01331)
*Hao Wang,Yiming Yao,Yaguang Xie,Tong Qiao,Zhidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的可解释图像隐写分析框架，用于零样本设置下的可逆图像隐藏方案，通过整合图像隐藏、揭示和隐写分析，并采用残差增强策略，显著提升了跨数据集和跨架构场景中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐写分析方法通常只能进行二分类且依赖训练与测试数据分布相似的假设，限制了其在现实场景中的应用，尤其难以应对可逆图像隐藏等新兴技术。

Method: 将图像隐藏、揭示和隐写分析整合到统一框架中，使隐写分析组件具备恢复嵌入秘密信息的能力；设计了一种简单有效的残差增强策略生成载体图像，提升模型在零样本下的泛化性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在可逆图像隐藏方案的隐写分析任务上显著优于现有技术，尤其在跨数据集和跨架构场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 所提框架不仅实现了对可逆隐藏图像的有效检测与信息恢复，还具备良好的可解释性和泛化能力，适用于更贴近实际应用的零样本隐写分析场景。

Abstract: Image steganalysis, which aims at detecting secret information concealed within images, has become a critical countermeasure for assessing the security of steganography methods, especially the emerging invertible image hiding approaches. However, prior studies merely classify input images into two categories (i.e., stego or cover) and typically conduct steganalysis under the constraint that training and testing data must follow similar distribution, thereby hindering their application in real-world scenarios. To overcome these shortcomings, we propose a novel interpretable image steganalysis framework tailored for invertible image hiding schemes under a challenging zero-shot setting. Specifically, we integrate image hiding, revealing, and steganalysis into a unified framework, endowing the steganalysis component with the capability to recover the secret information embedded in stego images. Additionally, we elaborate a simple yet effective residual augmentation strategy for generating stego images to further enhance the generalizability of the steganalyzer in cross-dataset and cross-architecture scenarios. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed approach significantly outperforms the existing steganalysis techniques for invertible image hiding schemes.

</details>


### [46] [Active Reasoning Vision-Language Models via Sequential Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01345)
*Anjie Liu,Ziqin Gong,Yan Song,Yuxiang Chen,Xiaolong Liu,Hengtong Lu,Kaike Zhang,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于序列贝叶斯最优实验设计（S-BOED）的框架，以解决视觉语言模型中视野广度与细节精度之间的权衡问题，并通过无需训练的推理策略在千兆像素级任务上显著提升现有模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉语言模型在处理高分辨率图像时面临感知带宽瓶颈，即扩大视野会损失细节，影响复杂推理能力。受主动视觉和信息觅食理论启发，需构建一种能动态权衡覆盖范围与分辨率的机制。

Method: 将视觉感知建模为序列决策过程，形式化为S-BOED问题，并推导出在连续千兆像素空间中可实现的近似解；提出一种无需训练、支持多种优化算法（如贪心采样与前瞻规划）的灵活推理策略。

Result: 在千兆像素级别基准测试中，该方法显著优于标准基线，提升了当前最先进模型的表现，并有效缩小了与人工标注oracle之间的差距。

Conclusion: 所提出的S-BOED框架为突破视觉语言模型的感知瓶颈提供了理论基础和实用方案，验证了结合主动感知策略提升复杂视觉推理的有效性。

Abstract: Visual perception in modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is constrained by a fundamental perceptual bandwidth bottleneck: a broad field of view inevitably sacrifices the fine-grained details necessary for complex reasoning. Inspired by the classical paradigms of active vision and information foraging, we frame overcoming this limitation as a sequential decision-making process. We formalise this process through the lens of the sequential Bayesian optimal experimental design (S-BOED) problem. While exact Bayesian inference is intractable in continuous gigapixel spaces, we derive principled yet tractable approximations that balance spatial coverage against resolution. To validate this framework, we present a training-free inference strategy as a practical instantiation of the S-BOED objective for agents equipped with multiple vision tools. Designed as a flexible template, this strategy accommodates arbitrary optimisation algorithms, ranging from efficient greedy sampling to look-ahead planning, to approximate the optimal design. Empirical evaluations on gigapixel-level benchmarks demonstrate that our approach further boosts the performance of state-of-the-art models, significantly outperforming standard baselines and effectively narrowing the gap towards human-annotated oracles.

</details>


### [47] [CHASE: Competing Hypotheses for Ambiguity-Aware Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01346)
*Kartik Jhawar,Yuhao Geng,Atul N. Parikh,Lipo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHASE是一种面向结构化模糊性的选择性预测框架，通过显式比较竞争性时序假设来判断是否做出决策或 abstain，相较于传统不确定性估计方法，在高模糊场景下显著提升了准确性与风险控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的选择性预测方法在部分可观测环境下因局部时序证据矛盾而导致置信度失真，难以有效识别真正的模糊性，因此需要一种能显式建模竞争性解释的机制。

Method: 提出CHASE框架，构建多个竞争性时序假设，并利用假设间的得分差距作为排序感知的选择器输入，优化全局决策边界以区分可安全承诺与本质不确定的情况。

Result: 在模拟和真实GUV视频实验中，CHASE在80%覆盖率下显著提升整体非放弃准确率、三类准确率和模糊对齐放弃率；在高模糊区域相对改进达11.0%（平均）和8.8%；在90%覆盖率下整体风险降低9.9%。

Conclusion: CHASE通过显式推理竞争假设，为结构化模糊环境下的决策提供了更可靠、更具鲁棒性的选择性预测方案。

Abstract: Standard selective prediction methods typically estimate uncertainty from the output of a single predictive branch. While effective for general uncertainty estimation, these approaches often struggle under partial observability, where local temporal evidence can be contradictory and standard confidence scores become misleading. We introduce CHASE (Competing Hypotheses for Ambiguity-Aware Selective Prediction), a selective prediction framework that explicitly compares structured temporal explanations to determine whether to commit to a decision or abstain. Because genuine ambiguity causes the score gap between competing hypotheses to collapse, CHASE optimizes a ranking-aware selector over these hypothesis margins to globally separate safe commitments from fundamentally uncertain ones. We evaluate this framework on the problem of hidden connectivity inference, utilizing a controlled, physically grounded simulator inspired by the dynamics of giant unilamellar vesicles (GUVs), alongside zero-shot qualitative transfer (without retraining or fine tuning) to representative real GUV videos. Our experiments demonstrate that explicitly reasoning over competing hypotheses provides a superior balance of metrics. Compared to canonical uncertainty baselines, CHASE achieves statistically significant gains in overall no-abstain accuracy, three-way accuracy, and overall ambiguity-aligned abstention (at 80% coverage). Specifically, it yields up to an 11.0% relative mean improvement in overall alignment, alongside up to an 8.8% relative boost in three-way accuracy in the very-high ambiguity regime. By maintaining a selective risk boundary strictly at par with the best baselines at 80% coverage, and reducing overall risk by 9.9% at 90% coverage, this framework offers a more reliable approach to decision-making under structured ambiguity.

</details>


### [48] [VoxAfford: Multi-Scale Voxel-Token Fusion for Open-Vocabulary 3D Affordance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01365)
*Haowen Sun,Shaolong Zhang,Mingyang Li,Chengzhong Ma,Xinzhe Chen,Qiongjie Cui,Xingyu Chen,Zeyang Liu,Xuguang Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VoxAfford方法，通过引入多尺度几何特征增强输出令牌，提升开放词汇3D功能检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法生成的输出令牌语义丰富但空间表征不足，难以有效支持3D定位。

Method: 利用冻结的预训练3D VQVAE编码器提取多尺度几何特征，通过交叉注意力机制将语义查询与对应体素尺度的几何模式匹配，并使用可学习的兼容性门控控制特征注入强度，最后通过语义条件注意力聚合生成空间感知的功能提示。

Result: 在开放词汇功能检测任务中达到最先进水平，mIoU提升约8%，并在真实机器人实验中验证了对新物体的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: VoxAfford有效弥补了自回归生成令牌在空间建模上的缺陷，显著提升了开放词汇3D功能检测的精度和实用性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D affordance detection requires localizing interaction regions on point clouds given novel affordance descriptions. Recent methods extend multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with special output tokens that are decoded into segmentation masks. However, these tokens are produced through autoregressive generation, which models sequential dependencies rather than spatial neighborhood relations, leaving them semantically rich but spatially impoverished for 3D localization. We propose Voxel-enhanced Affordance detection (VoxAfford), which bypasses this bottleneck by injecting multi-scale geometric features from a frozen pre-trained 3D VQVAE encoder into the output tokens after generation. Each output token uses its affordance semantics as a query to retrieve relevant geometric patterns from its paired voxel scale via cross-attention, with a learned compatibility gate controlling the injection strength. The enhanced tokens are then aggregated into a spatially-aware affordance prompt through semantic-conditioned attention and propagated alongside per-point features to generate the final mask. Experiments on open-vocabulary affordance detection tasks show that VoxAfford achieves state-of-the-art performance with approximately an 8% improvement in mIoU, and real robot experiments confirm zero-shot transfer to novel objects.

</details>


### [49] [VISTA: Video Interaction Spatio-Temporal Analysis Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01391)
*Alejandro Aparcedo,Akash Kumar,Aaryan Garg,Dalton Pham,Wen-Kai Chen,Anirudh Bharadwaj,Aman Chadha,Yogesh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是一个面向开放集、多实体和多动作的视频时空理解基准，通过分解视频中的实体、动作及关系动态，提供细粒度的多轴诊断，揭示现有视觉语言模型在时空理解上的局限与偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型基准主要针对简单单动作视频和封闭属性集，难以反映真实世界中复杂、自由形式的多实体多动作交互，且缺乏系统性的失败分析框架。

Method: 提出VISTA基准，将视频分解为可解释的实体、动作和关系动态，构建统一的交互感知分类体系，并整合多个数据集形成约1.2万个视频-查询对，支持对关系性、空间和时间理解的多维度评估。

Result: 在11个主流视觉语言模型上的系统评测表明，现有模型在多实体多动作理解上表现不佳，暴露出显著的时空理解偏差，传统指标难以发现这些缺陷。

Conclusion: VISTA是首个大规模、交互感知的视觉语言模型时空理解诊断基准，为模型设计、预训练策略和评估方法的进步提供了精细化的分析框架。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for Vision-Language Models (VLMs) primarily evaluate spatio-temporal understanding on simple single-action videos, closed attribute sets and restricted entity types, failing to capture the freeform, multi-action interactions between diverse entities which characterize real-world video understanding. Furthermore, the lack of a systematic framework for analyzing model failures across complementary spatio-temporal axes hinders comprehensive evaluation. To address these gaps, we introduce VISTA, a Video Interaction Spatio-Temporal Analysis benchmark designed for open-set, multi-entity and multi-action spatio-temporal understanding in VLMs. VISTA decomposes videos into interpretable entities, their associated actions, and relational dynamics, enabling multi-axis diagnostics and unified assessment of relational, spatial, and temporal understanding. Our benchmark integrates multiple datasets into a single interaction-aware taxonomy and comprises ~12K curated video-query pairs spanning diverse scenes and complexities. We systematically evaluate 11 state-of-the-art VLMs on VISTA, and break down aggregate performance across our taxonomy to reveal shortcomings and pronounced spatio-temporal biases obscured by traditional metrics. By providing detailed, taxonomy-driven diagnostics on a challenging dataset, VISTA offers a nuanced framework to guide advances in model design, pretraining strategies, and evaluation protocols. Overall, VISTA is the first, large-scale, interaction-aware diagnostic benchmark for spatio-temporal understanding in VLMs.

</details>


### [50] [Recall to Predict: Grounding Motion Forecasting in Interpretable Motion Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01393)
*Abhishek Vivekanandan,Ahmed Abouelazm,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可解释运动基元的端到端可微分运动预测框架，通过对比学习构建“运动银行”，利用锚点检索层和几何细化解码器实现高精度、多样且可解释的多模态轨迹预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动预测方法在可解释性与预测精度之间难以兼顾，基于锚点的方法存在潜在坍塌问题，而朴素轨迹采样限制了多模态多样性。

Method: 构建一个通过对比学习获得的物理可实现轨迹嵌入空间（即“运动银行”），设计锚点检索层，采用双层级门控交叉注意力机制和直通Gumbel-Softmax估计器进行动态检索与离散选择，并结合DETR风格解码器与WTA-GMM、潜变量多样性惩罚及软最小加权终点损失联合优化。

Result: 在Argoverse 2和Waymo Open Motion数据集上实现了具有竞争力的多模态预测精度，同时提升了模型可解释性，避免了传统隐变量查询的黑盒问题。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将预测建立在语义明确、可解释的运动基元之上，有效平衡了运动预测中的准确性、多样性与可解释性，为自动驾驶等应用提供了更可靠的决策基础。

Abstract: Motion forecasting often requires trading interpretability for predictive accuracy. Standard anchor-based architectures rely on opaque latent queries that are highly prone to latent collapse, or naive trajectory sampling that limits multi-modal diversity. We propose an end-to-end differentiable framework that grounds predictions in a comprehensive "motion bank", a structured embedding space of physically realizable trajectories constructed via contrastive learning. Rather than regressing paths from a blank slate, our architecture dynamically retrieves explicit motion priors using a novel Anchor Retrieval Layer. This module adapts orthogonally initialized queries via a Dual-Level Gated Cross-Attention mechanism and executes discrete trajectory selection using a Straight-Through Gumbel-Softmax estimator to preserve continuous gradient flow. The retrieved semantically grounded anchors are then geometrically refined by a DETR-style decoder, optimized jointly with a Winner-Takes-All (WTA) kinematic Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), a latent diversity penalty, and a soft-min weighted endpoint loss. By strictly conditioning the decoding phase on diverse, interpretable motion primitives, our approach eliminates the "black box" of standard latent queries while achieving competitive multi-modal accuracy on the Argoverse 2 and Waymo Open Motion datasets. Code is available at: https://github.com/abviv/recall2predict

</details>


### [51] [SplAttN: Bridging 2D and 3D with Gaussian Soft Splatting and Attention for Point Cloud Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01466)
*Zhaoyang Li,Zhichao You,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SplAttN，通过可微分高斯点阵化替代硬投影，解决多模态点云补全中的跨模态熵坍缩问题，增强了视觉先验传播和跨模态连接，实现了最先进的性能并验证了对视觉线索的稳健依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态点云补全方法中，标准硬投影导致稀疏支持，引发跨模态熵坍缩，阻碍视觉先验传播，限制了模态间的有效连接。

Method: 提出SplAttN，将投影重构为连续密度估计，采用可微分高斯点阵化生成密集、连续的图像平面表示，以改善梯度流动和跨模态连接的学习能力。

Result: 在PCN和ShapeNet-55/34上达到最先进性能；在KITTI基准上的反事实实验表明，SplAttN在移除视觉输入时仍保持对视觉线索的稳健依赖，而基线方法退化为单模态模板检索。

Conclusion: SplAttN有效解决了跨模态熵坍缩问题，建立了更强的跨模态连接，提升了多模态点云补全的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Although multi-modal learning has advanced point cloud completion, the theoretical mechanisms remain unclear. Recent works attribute success to the connection between modalities, yet we identify that standard hard projection severs this connection: projecting a sparse point cloud onto the image plane yields an extremely sparse support, which hinders visual prior propagation, a failure mode we term Cross-Modal Entropy Collapse. To address this practical limitation, we propose SplAttN, which replaces hard projection with Differentiable Gaussian Splatting to produce a dense, continuous image-plane representation. By reformulating projection as continuous density estimation, SplAttN avoids collapsed sparse support, facilitates gradient flow, and improves cross-modal connection learnability. Extensive experiments show that SplAttN achieves state-of-the-art performance on PCN and ShapeNet-55/34. Crucially, we utilize the real-world KITTI benchmark as a stress test for multi-modal reliance. Counter-factual evaluation reveals that while baselines degenerate into unimodal template retrievers insensitive to visual removal, SplAttN maintains a robust dependency on visual cues, validating that our method establishes an effective cross-modal connection. Code is available at https://github.com/zay002/SplAttN.

</details>


### [52] [IMPACT-HOI: Supervisory Control for Onset-Anchored Partial HOI Event Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01666)
*Haoshen Zhang,Di Wen,Kunyu Peng,David Schneider,Zeyun Zhong,Alexander Jaus,Zdravko Marinov,Jiale Wei,Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Yufeng Zhang,Yuanhao Luo,Lei Qi,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IMPACT-HOI是一个用于标注以自我为中心的程序性视频的混合主动框架，通过构建人类-物体交互（HOI）的结构化事件图来提供高质量的结构化监督，适用于从人类示范中学习机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决从人类示范中学习机器人操作时对高质量、结构化监督信号的需求，需要有效标注复杂的以自我为中心的程序性视频中的HOI事件。

Method: 该框架将标注任务建模为部分指定、起始锚定的事件状态的增量解析过程；采用信任校准控制器，在直接查询、人工确认建议和保守补全之间动态选择；并引入基于原子回滚的风险有界执行协议，保护已确认的人工决策不被自动更新覆盖。

Result: 用户研究显示，与基线相比，手动标注操作减少了13.5%，事件匹配率达到46.67%，且在研究协议下未发生任何已确认字段的冲突更新。

Conclusion: IMPACT-HOI能有效减少人工标注负担，同时保证标注一致性与可靠性，为构建面向机器人学习的高质量HOI数据集提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present IMPACT-HOI, a mixed-initiative framework for annotating egocentric procedural video by constructing structured event graphs for Human-Object Interactions (HOI), motivated by the need for high-quality structured supervision for learning robot manipulation from human demonstration. IMPACT-HOI frames this task as the incremental resolution of a partially specified, onset-anchored event state. A trust-calibrated controller selects among direct queries, human-confirmed suggestions, and conservative completions based on empirical annotator behavior and evidence quality. A risk-bounded execution protocol, utilizing atomic rollback, ensures that human-confirmed decisions are preserved against conflicting automated updates. A user study with 9 participants shows a 13.5% reduction in manual annotation actions, a 46.67% event match rate, and zero confirmed-field violations under the studied protocol. The code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/541741106/IMPACT_HOI.

</details>


### [53] [Decision Boundary-aware Generation for Long-tailed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01468)
*Jiacheng Yang,Ruichi Zhang,Chikai Shang,Mengke Li,Xinyi Shang,Junlong Gao,Yonggang Zhang,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Decision Boundary-aware Generation (DBG)的框架，通过生成靠近决策边界的样本，解决长尾数据中类别不平衡和决策边界模糊的问题，提升尾部类别的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾数据分布导致模型偏向头部类别，现有生成增强方法虽能缓解但会引入非局部特征混合，导致决策边界重叠和尾部类别分布偏移。

Method: 提出DBG框架，识别边界模糊问题，并通过生成有信息量的近边界样本来促进近边界表征学习，从而优化分类器的决策空间。

Result: 在多个标准长尾基准上，DBG consistently 提升了尾部类别和整体准确率，并减少了类别间的特征重叠。

Conclusion: DBG有效平衡了长尾数据的学习过程，增强了决策边界的可分性，为长尾学习提供了更优的生成增强方案。

Abstract: Long-tailed data bias decision boundaries toward head classes and degrade tail class accuracy. Diffusion-based generative augmentation address this problem by generating additional data, while head-to-tail transfer further mitigate the generator bias inherit from long-tailed dataset. However, we show that while head-to-tail transfer helps balance the decision space of the classifier, it also induces latent non-local feature mixing that entangles inter-class features, causing decision boundary overlap and tail class distribution shift. To address this, we first identify the problem of boundary ambiguity and then propose Decision Boundary-aware Generation (DBG) framework, which promotes near-boundary representation learning by generating informative near-boundary samples. Overall, DBG rebalances the long-tailed dataset while yielding more separable decision space for long-tailed learning. Across standard long-tailed benchmarks, DBG consistently improves tail class and overall accuracy with less inter-class overlap. The code of DBG is available at https://github.com/keepdigitalabc-svg/DBG.

</details>


### [54] [Hybrid Visual Telemetry for Bandwidth-Constrained Robotic Vision: A Pilot Study with HEVC Base Video and JPEG ROI Stills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01826)
*Natalia Trukhina,Vadim Vashkelis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双通道视觉遥测方案，通过低比特率视频流与选择性传输的高细节静态区域（ROI）结合，在带宽受限条件下提升机器人视觉的信息获取效率，并为后续采用JPEG AI等先进图像编码技术奠定方法基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带宽受限的机器人和监控系统中，单一压缩视频流难以同时满足场景感知与精细识别的需求，低码率视频常丢失关键细节，导致目标识别不可靠。为此，本文提出一种混合架构：低分辨率视频用于动态场景理解，事件驱动的高细节ROI用于精确识别。

Method: 构建一个双通道视觉传输系统，使用x265/HEVC编码低比特率连续视频作为基础流，辅以JPEG编码的高细节静态ROI图像进行局部增强；在总带宽约束下，比较纯视频与混合传输方案的性能，设计了基于无人机数据集、两种实际码率环境、多种ROI触发策略及对象级分类优化的实验协议。

Result: 实验证明，在相同通信预算下，混合传输方案相比仅使用视频的方法能更有效地支持下游机器感知任务，特别是在需要精细识别的场景中表现更优，验证了该范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了双通道视觉遥测的可行性与优势，为带宽受限系统中的机器人视觉提供了新的传输范式，并为后续引入如JPEG AI等语义图像编码技术作为高细节通道奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Bandwidth-constrained robotic and surveillance systems often rely on a single compressed video stream to support both continuous scene awareness and downstream machine perception. In practice, this creates a mismatch: low-bitrate video can preserve motion and coarse context, but often loses the fine local detail needed for reliable object recognition and decision-making. Motivated by a hybrid architecture in which low-resolution video supports dynamic scene understanding while eventdriven high-detail regions of interest (ROIs) support close-up identification and analytics, this paper formalizes a two-channel visual telemetry scheme in which a continuous low-bitrate video stream is augmented by selectively transmitted high-detail still ROIs. This first paper does not attempt to prove the superiority of a new still-image codec. Instead, it establishes the hybrid transmission paradigm itself using a practical and reproducible codec stack: x265/HEVC for the base video stream and JPEG stills for ROI refinement. We formulate the problem as bitrate-constrained information selection for robotic vision and define an experimental protocol in which video-only and hybrid schemes are compared under matched total communication budgets. The study is designed around UAV-oriented datasets, two practical bitrate regimes, several ROI triggering policies, and object-level classification refinement on selectively transmitted ROI stills. The resulting paper lays the methodological foundation for a second-stage investigation of JPEG AI as the semantic still-image channel within the same hybrid architecture.

</details>


### [55] [CSGuard: Toward Forgery-Resistant Watermarking in Diffusion Models via Compressed Sensing Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01479)
*Jiewei Lai,Lan Zhang,Chen Tang,Pengcheng Sun,Zhaopeng Zhang,Yunhao Wang,Hui Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CSGuard的抗伪造水印方案，利用压缩感知技术将水印生成与验证绑定到一个密钥矩阵上，有效防止未经授权用户进行伪造攻击，同时保持生成质量和水印完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于潜在空间的扩散模型水印方法容易受到伪造攻击，攻击者可通过逆向重建图像并重新生成实现错误归属，因此需要一种能够抵抗此类攻击的安全水印机制。

Method: 提出CSGuard，利用压缩感知理论设计水印框架，将水印的嵌入和验证过程与一个只有授权用户持有的秘密矩阵绑定，确保未授权用户无法正确提取或伪造水印。

Result: 实验结果表明，CSGuard将伪造攻击的成功率从100.0%降至28.12%，并对正常水印图像实现100%的检测率，在不牺牲水印效果的前提下显著提升了安全性。

Conclusion: CSGuard是首个针对扩散模型的抗伪造水印方案，能够在不影响生成质量和水印鲁棒性的前提下，有效防御伪造攻击，为数字内容版权保护提供了更安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Latent-based diffusion model watermarking embeds watermarks into generated images' latent space to enable content attribution, offering a training-free solution for intellectual property protection and digital forensics. However, these methods exhibit a critical vulnerability to the forgery attack, attackers can extract the watermark by inverting the watermarked image and re-generating it with an arbitrary prompt, thereby enabling false attribution on malicious content. In this paper, we propose the CSGuard, the first forgery-resistant watermarking schema that leverages compressed sensing to bind the watermarked image generation and verification to a secret matrix. This ensures that only users possessing the secret matrix can correctly embed or verify the image watermark, prevents the illegal users from forgery without compromising generation quality and watermark integrity. Experimental results demonstrate that CSGuard achieves strong forgery resistance, reduces the attack success rate from 100.0\% to 28.12\%, and achieve 100\% detection rate on benign watermarked images without compromising watermarking effectiveness.

</details>


### [56] [Hyp2Former: Hierarchy-Aware Hyperbolic Embeddings for Open-Set Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02580)
*Yao Lu,Rohit Mohan,Florian Drews,Yakov Miron,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Hyp2Former的端到端框架，用于开放集全景分割（OPS），通过在双曲空间中学习已知类别的层次语义相似性，有效识别未知物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPS方法通常将已知类别视为扁平标签集，忽略了语义层次结构对区分未知物体的重要作用。因此，需要一种能够利用层次语义先验来更好检测未知对象的方法。

Method: 提出Hyp2Former，一种无需在训练中显式建模未知类别的端到端框架，通过在双曲空间中连续学习已知类别之间的层次语义相似性，构建具有多级语义抽象的结构化嵌入空间。

Result: 在MS COCO、Cityscapes和Lost&Found等多个公开数据集上的实验表明，Hyp2Former在未知物体发现和分布内类别分割之间取得了最佳平衡，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 利用层次语义结构可以在不依赖未知样本训练的情况下有效提升未知物体的检测能力，Hyp2Former为开放集全景分割提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Recognizing unknown objects is crucial for safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving and robotics. Open-Set Panoptic Segmentation (OPS) aims to segment known thing and stuff classes while identifying valid unknown objects as separate instances. Prior OPS approaches largely treat known categories as a flat label set, ignoring the semantic hierarchy that provides valuable structural priors for distinguishing unknown objects from in-distribution classes. In this work, we propose Hyp2Former, an end-to-end framework for OPS that does not require explicit modeling of unknowns during training, and instead learns hierarchical semantic similarities continuously in hyperbolic space. By explicitly encoding hierarchical relationships among known categories, the model learns a structured embedding space that captures multiple levels of semantic abstraction. As a result, unknown objects that cannot be confidently classified as known categories still remain in close proximity to higher-level concepts (e.g., an unknown animal remains closer to "animal" or "object" than to unrelated concepts such as "electronics" or "stuff") and can therefore be reliably detected, even if their fine-grained category was not represented during training. Empirical evaluations across multiple public datasets such as MS COCO, Cityscapes, and Lost&Found demonstrate that Hyp2Former outperforms existing methods on OPS, achieving the best balance between unknown object discovery and in-distribution robustness.

</details>


### [57] [AttnRouter: Per-Category Attention Routing for Training-Free Image Editing on MMDiT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01480)
*Guandong Li,Mengxia Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的图像编辑方法KVInject，用于多模态扩散Transformer模型，在保持源图像结构方面优于传统方法，并通过AttnRouter实现按类别选择最优注意力操作，显著提升编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多模态扩散Transformer（MMDiT）上实现高效、无需训练的图像编辑，克服现有方法如MasaCtrl在该架构上的失效问题。

Method: 提出了KVInject方法，通过在特定层和去噪步长范围内将源图像的键/值投影与噪声部分融合；设计了AttnRouter路由机制，根据不同编辑类型选择最优注意力操作；并通过层、步长和alpha参数消融实验定位有效的编辑子电路。

Result: KVInject比MasaCtrl更简单且避免了提示不匹配问题；AttnRouter使用真实标签使综合评分提升6.4%，零样本分类器接近该性能的98%；早期去噪步骤（S0-7）中的K/V注入几乎恢复全部编辑增益，而早期或晚期层带无效；alpha取值[0.3, 0.5]为稳定区间。简单K/V缩放无法超越基线，激进变体导致生成崩溃。

Conclusion: KVInject结合AttnRouter为MMDiT架构提供了高效、无需训练的图像编辑方案，揭示了UNet经验中不可迁移的部分，明确了有效编辑的关键条件。

Abstract: We study training-free image editing on Qwen-Image-Edit-2511, a 60-block multi-modal diffusion transformer (MMDiT) that concatenates noise and source-image tokens within a single attention stream. We make three contributions. (i) We introduce KVInject, a single-forward attention manipulation that alpha-blends source-half key/value projections into the noise-half within a localized layer/step band. KVInject is simpler than the classical two-pass MasaCtrl recipe and avoids the prompt-mismatch failure mode that disables MasaCtrl on MMDiT (composite score drops 31% versus baseline). (ii) We show that no single attention operation dominates across edit types, motivating AttnRouter, a per-category routing table that dispatches edits to the operation that best preserves source structure for that type. With ground-truth categories the router improves the CLIP-T+DINO-I composite by 6.4% over the editing baseline; an automatic CLIP zero-shot classifier closes 98% of this gap despite only 55% category accuracy. (iii) Through layer-, step-, and alpha-band ablations we localize the editing-effective attention sub-circuit: K/V injection in early denoising steps (S0-7) recovers nearly all of the gain of full-step injection, while injection in early (L0-15) or late (L45-60) layer bands fails to drive editing entirely; alpha in [0.3, 0.5] is a stable sweet spot. We also report negative results that highlight what does not transfer from the UNet folklore: simple K/V rescaling never beats baseline and aggressive variants collapse generation entirely (composite 0.084). We release code, pre-computed routing tables, and a 100-sample stratified subset of ImgEdit-Bench used in all ablations.

</details>


### [58] [Seeing Realism from Simulation: Efficient Video Transfer for Vision-Language-Action Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02757)
*Chenyu Hui,Xiaodi Huang,Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Shan You,Fei Wang,Tao Huang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的视频增强框架，将模拟的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）视频转化为逼真的训练视频，有效缩小了模拟与真实世界之间的域差距，并在多个基准上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟数据虽易于大规模采集，但存在明显的视觉域差距和环境多样性不足，导致在真实场景中泛化能力弱。因此需要一种能保留任务语义的同时提升模拟视频真实性和多样性的方法。

Method: 通过视频语义分割和视频描述提取模拟视频中的结构化条件，重写描述以增加环境多样性，并利用条件视频转换模型合成逼真视频；引入扩散特征重用机制加速生成，并采用核心集采样策略在计算受限下选择最具代表性的视频进行增强。

Result: 在Robotwin 2.0、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus及真实机器人平台上实验表明，该方法显著提升模型性能，例如在Robotwin 2.0上使RDT-1B提升8%，在LIBERO-Plus上使$π_0$提升5.1%。

Conclusion: 所提出的视频增强框架能高效地将模拟VLA视频转化为多样化且逼真的训练数据，显著增强模型在真实世界中的泛化能力，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically rely on large-scale real-world videos, whereas simulated data, despite being inexpensive and highly parallelizable to collect, often suffers from a substantial visual domain gap and limited environmental diversity, resulting in weak real-world generalization. We present an efficient video augmentation framework that converts simulated VLA videos into realistic training videos while preserving task semantics and action trajectories. Our pipeline extracts structured conditions from simulation via video semantic segmentation and video captioning, rewrites captions to diversify environments, and uses a conditional video transfer model to synthesize realistic videos. To make augmentation practical at scale, we introduce a diffusion feature-reuse mechanism that reuses video tokens across adjacent timesteps to accelerate generation, and a coreset sampling strategy that identifies a compact, non-redundant subset for augmentation under limited computation. Extensive experiments on Robotwin 2.0, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and a real robotic platform demonstrate consistent improvements. For example, our method improves RDT-1B by 8% on Robotwin 2.0, and boosts $π_0$ by 5.1% on the more challenging LIBERO-Plus benchmark. Code is available at: https://github.com/nanfangxiansheng/Seeing-Realism-from-Simulation.

</details>


### [59] [CGFformer: Cluster-Guidance Frequency Transformer for Pansharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01490)
*Zijian Zhou,Jianing Zhang,Kai Sun,Xiangyu Zhao,Chunxia Zhang,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CGFformer的聚类引导频率Transformer模型，用于解决现有全色锐化方法中固定频率滤波器无法自适应复杂频率分布以及去噪能力不足的问题。该方法通过自适应分离模块、双流精炼模块和频率-空间融合模块，实现了更精确的高频/低频分离、有效去噪及空间-频率交互，显著提升了多光谱图像融合质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于频率的全色锐化方法使用固定的频率滤波器，难以适应PAN和MS图像中复杂且空间变化的频率分布；同时，现有去噪策略对频率成分利用不充分，难以准确抑制多种噪声类型。因此需要一种能动态适应频率分布并有效去除混合噪声的方法。

Method: 提出CGFformer模型：1）设计基于K-means聚类的自适应分离模块，结合局部特征与非局部信息，实现高精度高低频分量分离；2）引入基于Transformer交叉注意力的双流精炼模块，联合抑制频率相关和无关干扰；3）构建频率-空间融合模块，增强细节重建并促进空间与频率信息交互。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明CGFformer在客观指标和视觉质量方面均优于现有的全色锐化方法，尤其在细节恢复和噪声抑制方面表现突出。

Conclusion: CGFformer通过聚类引导的频率分解与Transformer驱动的双流去噪，有效解决了传统方法在频率适应性和去噪能力上的局限，显著提升了全色锐化性能，为遥感图像融合提供了新的高效框架。

Abstract: Pansharpening aims to generate high-resolution multispectral (HRMS) images by fusing low-resolution multispectral (LRMS) images with high-resolution panchromatic (PAN) images. However, the current mainstream frequency-based pansharpening methods employ fixed frequency filters, which cannot precisely adapt to complex and spatially diversified frequency distributions in PAN and MS images. Furthermore, existing denoising strategies insufficiently exploit frequency components for denoising and struggle to suppress various noise types accurately. To address these challenges, we propose CGFformer, a cluster-guidance frequency Transformer that focuses on varying frequency distribution and interactions between frequency and spatial components. Specifically, we design an adaptive separation module that integrates local features and non-local information through K-means clustering, enabling more precise separation of high- and low-frequency components. Subsequently, we introduce a dual-stream refinement module combined with Transformer-based cross-attention to remove various noise, allowing the network to jointly suppress frequency-relevant and irrelevant disturbances. In addition, we develop a frequency-spatial fusion module designed to enhance detail and facilitate spatial-frequency interaction, ensuring more effective reconstruction of spatial structures in the fused results. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed CGFformer achieves notable improvements over existing pansharpening approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [60] [H-Probes: Extracting Hierarchical Structures From Latent Representations of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00847)
*Cutter Dawes,Aryan Sharma,Angelos Ioannis Lagos,Shivam Raval*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了H-probes，一种用于从大语言模型的潜在表征中提取层次结构（如深度和成对距离）的线性探针方法，并在合成树遍历任务和真实世界场景中验证了模型对层次结构的几何表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在多种需要层次推理的任务中表现出色，但其内部如何几何化表示所需层次结构仍缺乏分析。为此，作者希望揭示模型中潜在的层次结构表征机制。

Method: 提出H-probes，即一组线性探针，用于从模型的潜在表示中提取层次结构信息（如节点深度和距离）。在合成树遍历任务上进行测试，并通过全面的消融实验分析这些子空间的维度、因果重要性和泛化能力。同时在数学推理等真实层次任务中验证发现。

Result: H-probes能稳健地识别出完成任务所需的层次结构子空间；这些子空间低维、对高性能有因果作用，并具域内和跨域泛化性；在数学推理等真实场景中也发现了较弱但类似的层次结构。

Conclusion: 大语言模型不仅在句法和概念层面，而且在更抽象的推理过程本身中，都存在对层次结构的几何表示，H-probes为理解模型的层级推理机制提供了新工具。

Abstract: Representing and navigating hierarchy is a fundamental primitive of reasoning. Large language models have demonstrated proficiency in a wide variety of tasks requiring hierarchical reasoning, but there exists limited analysis on how the models geometrically represent the necessary latent constructions for such thinking. To this end, we develop \textit{H-probes}, a collection of linear probes that extract hierarchical structure, specifically depth and pairwise distance, from latent representations. In synthetic tree traversal tasks, the H-probes robustly find the subspaces containing hierarchical structure necessary to complete the tasks; furthermore, in comprehensive ablation experiments, we show that these hierarchy-containing subspaces are low-dimensional, causally important for high task performance, and generalize within- and out-of-domain. Furthermore, we find analogous, though weaker, hierarchical structure in real-world hierarchical contexts such as mathematical reasoning traces. These results demonstrate that models represent hierarchy not only at the level of syntax and concepts, but at deeper levels of abstraction -- including the reasoning process itself.

</details>


### [61] [Model Organisms Are Leaky: Perplexity Differencing Often Reveals Finetuning Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00994)
*Mohammed Abu Baker,Luca Baroni,Dan Wilhelm*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于困惑度的简单方法，通过利用微调模型在非预期上下文中过度泛化其行为的特性，从模型“模式生物”中揭示微调目标，该方法仅需微调模型的下一个词概率，适用于API受限但暴露token logprobs的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现微调可能引入有害或不安全行为，为了控制实验研究这些风险，需要识别出微调后模型所表现出的具体已知行为（即模型“模式生物”），但当前识别这些行为仍具挑战性。

Method: 首先使用来自通用语料库的短随机前缀生成微调模型的多样化补全；然后根据参考模型与微调模型之间的困惑度差异对补全结果进行排序，高排名的结果往往能揭示微调目标，且无需访问模型内部结构或先验假设。

Result: 在76个不同规模（0.5至70B参数）和类型的模型“模式生物”上验证了该方法的有效性，包括后门模型、通过合成文档微调植入错误事实的模型、具有隐藏不良行为的对抗训练模型以及出现新兴不对齐行为的模型；大多数情况下，该方法能在高排名结果中揭示微调目标，尤其对合成文档微调或需输出特定短语的模型更敏感；即使没有原始预微调检查点，也可用其他家族的可信参考模型替代。

Conclusion: 该困惑度-based方法是一种轻量、通用且实用的技术，可用于检测语言模型中的隐式微调行为，适用于黑盒API场景，有助于提升模型安全性与可解释性。

Abstract: Finetuning can significantly modify the behavior of large language models, including introducing harmful or unsafe behaviors. To study these risks, researchers develop model organisms: models finetuned to exhibit specific known behaviors for controlled experimentation. Identifying these behaviors remains challenging. We show that a simple perplexity-based method can surface finetuning objectives from model organisms by leveraging their tendency to overgeneralize their finetuned behaviors beyond the intended context. First, we generate diverse completions from the finetuned model using short random prefills drawn from general corpora. Second, we rank completions by decreasing perplexity gap between reference and finetuned models. The top-ranked completions often reveal the finetuning objectives, without requiring model internals or prior assumptions about the behavior. We evaluate this on a diverse set of model organisms (N=76, 0.5 to 70B parameters), including backdoored models, models finetuned to internalize false facts via synthetic document finetuning, adversarially trained models with hidden concerning behaviors, and models exhibiting emergent misalignment. For the vast majority of model organisms tested, the method surfaces completions revealing finetuning objectives within the top-ranked results, with models trained via synthetic document finetuning or to produce exact phrases being particularly susceptible. We further show that the technique can be effective even without access to the exact pre-finetuning checkpoint: trusted reference models from different families can serve as effective substitutes. As the method requires only next-token probabilities from the finetuned model, it is compatible with API-gated models that expose token logprobs.

</details>


### [62] [Can AI Debias the News? LLM Interventions Improve Cross-Partisan Receptivity but LLMs Overestimate Their Own Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01006)
*Faisal Feroz,Jonas R. Kunst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLM）可用于去偏见化自由派新闻标题，从而提升保守派读者的信任度，但仅在涉及意识形态框架重构时有效，表面词汇调整无效；此外，LLM模拟结果与人类反应存在偏差，表明当前模型仍需人类监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索LLM是否能有效减轻党派新闻媒体对跨党派信任的侵蚀，特别是通过去偏见化处理自由派新闻标题来改善保守派读者的信任判断。

Method: 进行了两项预注册实验：研究1测试了词汇层面的去情绪化替换对读者判断的影响；研究2测试了更深层次的框架重构干预效果，并比较了人类参与者与LLM模拟‘硅基参与者’的反应差异。

Result: 研究1发现词汇去偏见化对人类读者无显著影响，但在LLM模拟中效果显著；研究2发现框架重构显著提升了保守派对新闻可信度、完整性和互动意愿的评价，且未在自由派中引发逆火效应；LLM预测的方向与人类一致，但效应量更大，且其对响应人群的预测与实际心理特征不符。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的去偏见化在调整意识形态框架时可有效提升跨党派接受度，但当前模型在量化准确性和心理保真度上仍有不足，必须依赖人类监督来评估干预效果。

Abstract: Partisan news media erode cross-partisan trust, but large language models (LLMs) offer a potential means of debiasing such content at scale. Across two pre-registered experiments, we tested whether LLM-generated debiasing of liberal news headlines could improve conservative readers' trust-relevant judgments. Study 1 found that subtle lexical debiasing (replacing emotive words with more moderate synonyms) had no effect on any outcome. Study 2 found that a more substantive reframing intervention significantly increased conservatives' perceived trustworthiness, completeness, and willingness to engage with liberal news headlines, without producing a backfire effect among a sample of liberals. In Study 1, the intervention produced robust effects among LLM-simulated silicon participants, whereas it had no impact on human readers. In Study 2, the intervention's effects among silicon participants aligned directionally with human responses but were significantly larger in magnitude for some outcomes. Moderation analyses revealed that the model's implicit theory of who responds to debiasing diverged from the psychological profile that actually predicted human responsiveness. These findings demonstrate that LLM-based debiasing can improve cross-partisan receptivity when targeting ideological framing rather than surface-level language, but that current models lack both the quantitative accuracy and qualitative psychological fidelity to evaluate their own interventions without human oversight.

</details>


### [63] [CLEAR: Revealing How Noise and Ambiguity Degrade Reliability in LLMs for Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01011)
*Kevin H. Guo,Chao Yan,Avinash Baidya,Katherine Brown,Xiang Goa,Juming Xiong,Zhijun Yin,Bradley A. Malin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CLEAR框架，用于评估决策空间呈现、模糊性和不确定性对医学大语言模型（LLM）推理能力的影响。研究发现，当前医学LLM评估方法存在局限性，增加合理选项数量、引入‘我不知道’类选项以及模型规模扩大均会加剧模型的“谦逊缺失”问题，即模型难以正确识别答案并避免错误选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学LLM评估依赖于简化的考试式基准，无法反映真实世界医疗问题中的模糊性和不确定性，因此需要更贴近临床实际的评估方法。

Method: 提出CLEAR框架，通过系统性扰动三个因素来评估LLM表现：(1) 可能答案的数量；(2) 是否包含标准答案或拒绝回答选项；(3) 答案选项的语义表述方式。在三个基准上对17个LLM进行测试。

Result: 实验表明：增加合理答案数量会降低模型识别正确答案和拒绝错误答案的能力；当‘拒绝回答’选项从‘以上皆非’变为‘我不知道’时，模型更倾向于选择错误答案；并且模型规模越大，其‘谦逊缺失’（正确选择与拒绝错误之间的性能差距）越严重。

Conclusion: 当前的医学LLM评估方法存在缺陷，仅靠模型扩展无法解决可靠性问题，需重视模型在不确定情况下的审慎决策能力。

Abstract: Medical large language model (LLM) evaluations rely on simplified, exam-style benchmarks that rarely reflect the ambiguity of real-world medical inquiries. We introduce the CLinical Evaluation of Ambiguity and Reliability (CLEAR) framework, which assesses how decision-space presentation, ambiguity, and uncertainty affect LLMs' reasoning on medical benchmarks. CLEAR systematically perturbs (1) the number of plausible answer options, (2) the presence of a ground truth or abstention option, and (3) the semantic framing of answer options. Applying CLEAR on three benchmarks evaluated across 17 LLMs reveals three notable limitations of existing evaluation methods. First, increasing the number of plausible answers degrades a model's ability to identify the correct answer and abstain against incorrect ones. Second, this lack of caution intensifies as the framing of abstention shifts from assertive rejection like "None of the Above" to uncertainty admission like "I don't know" (IDK). Notably, just including IDK in the answer space increases incorrect answer selections. Lastly, we formalize the performance gap between identifying the correct answer and abstaining from incorrect ones as the humility deficit, which worsens with model scale. Our findings reveal limitations in standard medical benchmarks and underscore that scaling alone does not resolve LLM reliability issues.

</details>


### [64] [Psychologically Potent, Computationally Invisible: LLMs Generate Social-Comparison Triggers They Fail to Detect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01017)
*Hua Zhao,Jiapei Gu,Michelle Mingyue Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了XHS-SCoRE，一个基于读者视角的基准，用于检测小红书纯文本帖子是否引发向上、向下或中性社会比较，并揭示提示式大模型在识别此类社会关系信号时存在稳定性与可解释性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在捕捉小红书内容中真实存在的社会比较效应，该信号具有行为意义且独立于情感倾向，但现有基于提示的大语言模型难以可靠识别此类读者层面的隐含关系。

Method: 构建了一个以读者为中心的标注数据集XHS-SCoRE，采用提示式大模型分类器和监督式中文编码器基线模型进行实验，并通过受控试点研究验证生成内容对感知地位的影响。

Result: 发现提示式大模型虽生成流畅但检测能力不稳定，表现出将比较性内容中性化及模型特异的方向性偏差；而监督模型显示信号可学习但提示推理不稳健。

Conclusion: XHS-SCoRE提供了一个评估读者导向社会比较检测的新基准，并表明当前提示式推理方法尚不能充分捕捉某些深层社会关系信号。

Abstract: We introduce Xiaohongshu Social Comparison Reader Elicitation (XHS-SCoRE), a reader-grounded benchmark for detecting if a text-only Xiaohongshu (RedNote) post elicits UPWARD, DOWNWARD, or NEUTRAL/no clear social comparison from a first-person reader perspective. The task targets a socially meaningful relational signal that is behaviorally real yet not reducible to sentiment. Across prompted LLM classifiers and supervised Chinese encoder baselines, we find a consistent mismatch between generation fluency and reliable detection ability: the signal is textually learnable in-domain, but not robustly accessible to prompt-based classification. Prompted LLM classifiers exhibit stable, interpretable failure modes, especially neutralization of comparison-triggering posts and model-specific directional skew. A controlled pilot further shows that LLM-generated Xiaohongshu-style posts can shift perceived standing and comparison-related affect even when prompt-based detection of the same construct remains fragile. XHS-SCoRE contributes both a benchmark for reader-grounded comparison detection and a diagnostic framework for studying when socially meaningful relational cues remain only partially visible to prompt-based inference.

</details>


### [65] [A Theoretical Game of Attacks via Compositional Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01034)
*Xinbo Wu,Huan Zhang,Abhishek Umrawal,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种理论框架来形式化大语言模型对齐中的攻防博弈，揭示了攻击方的内在优势，并设计了理论上最优的攻防策略，在实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型能力增强，安全部署问题日益突出。尽管已有多种对齐策略，但仍易被精心设计的对抗性提示绕过，因此需要更系统的理论分析来理解攻防本质。

Method: 构建了一个攻击者与防御者之间的博弈论框架，提出了理论上的最优响应攻击策略，并分析了该博弈的均衡特性，推导出可证明最优的防御策略。同时在多种大模型和基准上进行了实证评估。

Result: 理论分析表明攻击方具有内在优势；提出的最佳响应攻击在实践中表现出优于现有对抗提示方法的效果；并得到了一种可证明最优的防御方案。

Conclusion: 本文通过博弈论视角揭示了当前大模型对齐机制中攻防不对称的本质，为设计更鲁棒的防御提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: As large language models grow increasingly capable, concerns about their safe deployment have intensified. While numerous alignment strategies aim to restrict harmful behavior, these defenses can still be circumvented through carefully designed adversarial prompts. In this work, we introduce a theoretical framework that formalizes a game between an attacker and a defender. Within this framework, we design a theoretical best-response attack strategy and show that it is closely related to many existing adversarial prompting methods. We further analyze the resulting game, characterize its equilibria, and reveal inherent advantages for the attacker. Drawing on our theoretical analysis, we also derive a provably optimal defense strategy. Empirically, we evaluate a practical instantiation of the theoretically optimal attack and observe stronger performance relative to existing adversarial prompting approaches in diverse settings encompassing different LLMs and benchmarks.

</details>


### [66] [Compared to What? Baselines and Metrics for Counterfactual Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01048)
*Zihao Yang,Mosh Levy,Yoav Goldberg,Byron C. Wallace*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的框架，通过与改写输入的基线变化进行统计比较，来更准确地衡量大语言模型对特定干预（如性别、风格线索）的敏感性。研究发现，许多先前报告的模型敏感性在考虑了通用模型敏感性后显著减弱，但该框架仍能检测到真实的微小方向性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实提示方法无法区分目标因素的影响和文本表面形式变化带来的影响，导致可能错误归因模型行为。为了正确评估模型对特定变量的敏感性，需要控制基线的非语义改变所引起的变化。

Method: 提出一个统计测试框架，将目标干预下的输出变化与输入改写（paraphrasing）引起的输出变化进行对比；使用每样本分布指标和回归分析来增强检测效力，并评估不同度量方式的效果。

Result: 在MedQA数据集上，改变患者性别导致14.9%的预测翻转率，与改写输入导致的14.1%无显著差异；在MedPerturb数据集中，原本报告的多数敏感性效应在校正后消失，仅5/120测试显著；而在职业传记分类任务中成功检测到显著的方向性性别偏见。

Conclusion: 反事实提示的结果必须与基线改写效果比较才能可靠解释；提出的框架有助于识别真实且具有方向性的模型偏差，避免将表面变化误认为语义敏感性。

Abstract: Counterfactual prompting (i.e., perturbing a single factor and measuring output change) is widely used to evaluate things like LLM bias and CoT faithfulness. But in this work we argue that observed effects cannot be attributed to the targeted factor without accounting for baseline ``meaning-preserving'' modifications to text that establish general model sensitivity. This is because every counterfactual edit is a compound treatment that bundles the variable of interest with incidental surface-form variation; this violates treatment variation irrelevance. We observe prediction flip rates on MedQA of 14.9% when we surgically change patient gender. However, this is statistically indistinguishable from the flip rates induced by simply paraphrasing inputs (14.1%). In this case, it would therefore be unwarranted to conclude that the LLM is especially sensitive to patient gender. To account for this and robustly measure the effects of targeted interventions, we propose a framework in which we compare (via statistical testing) differences observed under target interventions to those induced by paraphrasing inputs. We then use this framework to revisit a analysis done on the MedPerturb dataset, which reported evidence of model sensitivity to patient demographics and stylistic cues. We find that these effects largely dissipate when we account for general model sensitivity, with only 5 of 120 tests reaching statistical significance. Applying the same framework to occupational biography classification, we detect clearly significant directional gender bias, showing that the framework identifies real directional effects even when they are small. We evaluate a range of metrics -- aggregate, per-sample distributional, and regression -- and find that per-sample metrics are dramatically more powerful than aggregate metrics and regression powerfully and uniquely characterizes effect direction and magnitude.

</details>


### [67] [A Systematic Exploration of Text Decomposition and Budget Distribution in Differentially Private Text Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01065)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Angelo Kleinert,Florian Matthes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了在差分隐私文本混淆中不同的文本分解和预算分配技术，揭示了设计选择对隐私保护效果的重要影响，并证明了通过优化差分隐私混淆过程来最大化实证权衡的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在保持文本可用性的同时实现文档级别的隐私保护，需要研究如何合理分配差分隐私预算到文本的不同部分。

Method: 通过实验比较多种文本分块方法与隐私预算分配策略的组合，分析其在相同隐私预算下对输出文本质量和隐私保护的影响。

Result: 实验表明，不同的文本分块和预算分配方法即使在相似的隐私预算下也会产生显著不同的结果，说明设计选择至关重要。

Conclusion: 优化差分隐私文本混淆中的文本分解和预算分配策略，能够有效提升隐私与效用之间的实证权衡，验证了该方向的可行性。

Abstract: The goal of differentially private text obfuscation is to obfuscate, or "perturb", input texts with Differential Privacy (DP) guarantees, such that the private output texts are quantifiably indistinguishable from the originals. While perturbation at the word level is intuitive, meaningful text privatization happens on complete documents. Recent research has laid the groundwork for reasoning about privacy budget distribution, namely, how an overall $\varepsilon$ budget can be sensibly distributed among the component pieces of a text. We perform a systematic evaluation of multiple text decomposition and budget distribution techniques in the context of DP text obfuscation, testing how different methods for chunking texts can be combined with techniques for allocating $\varepsilon$ to these chunks. Our experiments reveal that such design choices are very important, as even with comparable privacy budgets, significantly different results can occur based on which methods are chosen. In this, we provide credible evidence of the feasibility of maximizing empirical trade-offs by optimizing DP obfuscation procedures.

</details>


### [68] [Controlled Paraphrase Geometry in Sentence Embedding Space: Local Manifold Modeling and Latent Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01073)
*Leonid Bedratyuk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了由语义相近句子生成的句嵌入空间中的局部几何结构，提出基于仿射、二次和三次拟合模型的局部几何建模方法，并引入表面引导的潜在探测技术生成合成隐变量点，通过多项指标验证其几何一致性。实验表明非线性模型优于仿射模型，但几何有效性并不直接提升分类性能，强调需区分几何有效性与判别效用。同时贡献了一个大规模可控数据集CoPaGE-300K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索句子嵌入空间中语义相近句子的局部几何组织方式，理解受控语义变化如何在嵌入空间中体现，并检验是否可用低阶模型显式刻画该结构。

Method: 提出基于affine、quadratic和cubic模型的局部几何建模方案；采用基于主曲面的潜在探测方法，在局部PCA降维空间内构造符合拟合载体的合成隐变量点，用于表示空间分析与几何感知探测。

Result: 非线性模型（二次、三次）比仿射模型更准确地描述嵌入云；生成的隐变量点在表面一致性、Hessian形状稳定性和系数稳定性方面表现良好；但几何上有效的点未显著提升下游分类任务性能。

Conclusion: 支持对句子嵌入空间进行显式的局部几何建模，但指出几何有效性不等同于判别效用，需在表征分析中加以区分；所提方法适用于嵌入空间的离线分析，且CoPaGE-300K为相关研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: The paper studies the local geometry of embedding clouds induced by \emph{controlled local classes of semantically close sentences}. The central question is how controlled paraphrase-like semantic variation is organized in sentence embedding space and whether this local structure can be explicitly modeled by low-degree fitted carriers.
  We introduce a local geometric modeling scheme based on affine, quadratic, and cubic fitted models. We also use a surface-based latent probing procedure that constructs synthetic latent points in a reduced local PCA space with respect to the fitted carrier. The procedure is intended as an offline method for representation-space analysis, local manifold modeling, and geometry-aware latent probing.
  Generated latent points are evaluated using criteria that measure consistency with the fitted surface, preservation of neighborhood structure, agreement with the empirical distribution, stability of Hessian-based second-order shape descriptors, and stability of fitted-model coefficients. Experiments on controlled sets of semantically close sentences show that nonlinear local models describe embedding clouds more accurately than affine models. Surface-based generation provides strong fitted-geometry fidelity, including surface consistency, Hessian-based shape consistency, and coefficient consistency.
  Downstream experiments show that geometric validity of synthetic latent points does not automatically translate into improved classification performance. The results support explicit local geometric modeling of sentence embedding space and highlight the need to distinguish geometric validity from discriminative utility. As a resource contribution, we introduce \textbf{CoPaGE-300K}, a controlled template-based dataset of semantically close sentence variants with slot-level annotations and precomputed sentence embeddings.

</details>


### [69] [Teaching LLMs Brazilian Healthcare: Injecting Knowledge from Official Clinical Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01077)
*Hugo Abonizio,Filipe Rocha Lopes,Roberto Lotufo,Rodrigo Nogueira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对巴西葡萄牙语临床指南知识的大型语言模型性能不足问题，提出了一种基于Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct的适应性方法，并构建了新的评估基准HealthBench-BR和PCDT-QA。通过合成数据训练与强化学习优化，模型在仅有140亿参数的情况下超越了更大的商用模型，同时公开了全部数据与模型以促进可重复研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在巴西统一卫生系统（SUS）官方临床指南的知识掌握上表现不佳，且缺乏针对巴西葡萄牙语临床协议的评估基准，限制了临床自然语言处理在该语种中的应用与发展。

Method: 从178份官方指南中生成约7000万标记的合成数据，包含改写、百科文章和问答三种格式，使用四个生成型大模型确保多样性；采用持续预训练结合组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行模型微调。

Result: 提出了HealthBench-BR（1,780个真/假判断题）和PCDT-QA（890个开放性问题，由LLM评分）两个新基准；最佳模型在HealthBench-BR上达到83.9%，在PCDT-QA上达到85.4%，优于GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro及Google AI Overview的RAG系统。

Conclusion: 通过多样化的合成数据生成和强化学习优化，可在较小参数规模下实现对特定国家临床指南的高效建模，验证了领域适配与专用基准的重要性，推动了巴西葡萄牙语临床NLP的发展。

Abstract: Brazil's Unified Health System (SUS) relies on official clinical guidelines that define diagnostic criteria, treatments, dosages, and monitoring procedures for over 200 million citizens. Yet current LLMs perform poorly on this guideline-specific knowledge, and no benchmark evaluates clinical recall grounded in Brazilian Portuguese protocols. We address this gap by adapting Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct to the Brazilian clinical domain. From 178 official guidelines (~5.4M tokens), we generate ~70M tokens of synthetic data in three formats -- rephrases, wiki-style articles, and question-answer pairs -- using four generator LLMs. We then apply continual pre-training followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). We introduce HealthBench-BR, with 1,780 balanced true/false clinical assertions, and PCDT-QA, with 890 open-ended clinical questions scored by an LLM judge. Our best model achieves 83.9% on HealthBench-BR and 85.4% on PCDT-QA, outperforming GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and Google AI Overview's web-grounded RAG despite having only 14B parameters. Ablations show that generator diversity and reinforcement learning are critical to these gains. We release all datasets, benchmarks, and model weights to support reproducible clinical NLP research for Brazilian Portuguese. Code, data, and model weights are available at https://github.com/hugoabonizio/clinical-protocols-br

</details>


### [70] [Interpretable Difficulty-Aware Knowledge Tracing in Tutor-Student Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01097)
*Shuyan Huang,Alexander Scarlatos,Jaewook Lee,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型的可解释性对话式知识追踪框架，结合项目反应理论，显式建模学生能力和题目难度，提升预测准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式知识追踪方法忽略题目难度建模且依赖黑箱式大语言模型表征，导致预测不够准确和难以解释，因此需要一种更可解释、更精准的对话环境下的知识追踪方法。

Method: 构建一个基于大语言模型的可解释性难度感知对话式知识追踪框架，利用原始问题文本和下一教学任务来估计学生知识状态和未来任务难度，并结合项目反应理论将大语言模型输出映射为学生能力与题目难度参数。

Result: 在两个师生对话数据集上实验表明，该框架优于现有的知识追踪基线方法，同时生成符合认知学习理论的可解释结果。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过融合大语言模型与认知理论，在对话环境中实现了更准确且可解释的学生表现预测，推动了AI个性化辅导系统的发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have led to the development of AI-powered tutoring systems that provide interactive support via dialogue. To enable these tutoring systems to provide personalized support, it is essential to assess student performance at each turn, motivating knowledge tracing (KT) in dialogue settings. However, existing dialogue-based KT approaches often ignore question difficulty modeling and rely on opaque latent representations from LLMs, hindering accurate and interpretable prediction. In this work, we propose an interpretable difficulty-aware conversational KT framework built upon LLMs, which explicitly models students' abilities and the difficulty of tutor-posed tasks at each turn. The framework incorporates the original textual question and the next tutor-posed task to estimate the student's knowledge state and the difficulty of the upcoming turn. Furthermore, it integrates Item Response Theory to map LLM's outputs into student ability and question difficulty parameters, enabling interpretable prediction of student performance grounded in cognitive theories of learning. We evaluate the framework on two tutor-student dialogue datasets. Both quantitative and qualitative results show that our framework outperforms existing KT baselines, meanwhile generating interpretable outputs consistent with cognitive theory.

</details>


### [71] [Quantifying and Predicting Disagreement in Graded Human Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01168)
*Leixin Zhang,Çağrı Çöltekin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在不当语言（包括冒犯性语言、仇恨言论和有毒语言）的人类分级标注中，标注分歧的模式，并提出了一种量化标注者观点对立程度的“对立指数”，发现标注方差与文本特征之间存在中等正相关，且高对立指数的项目更难被模型准确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人类标注者在许多任务中存在固有分歧，而不同样本引发的分歧程度不同，因此需要探究不当语言标注中的分歧模式，并预测哪些样本可能导致强烈的意见对立。

Method: 分析分级标注数据中的变异模式，提出‘对立指数’来量化标注者之间的观点对立，并利用文本特征预测标注方差和高对立指数实例，比较直接预测方差与通过预测分布估计方差两种方法的性能。

Result: 标注方差的估计值与观测值之间存在中等正相关；两种方差预测方法表现相当；高对立指数的样本更难预测，且常被模型低估。

Conclusion: 标注分歧可从文本特征中得到一定程度的预测，但高对立性的样本更具挑战性，需进一步建模以捕捉多元观点。

Abstract: It is increasingly recognized that human annotators do not always agree, and such disagreement is inherent in many annotation tasks. However, not all instances in a given task elicit the same degree of opinion divergence. In this paper, we investigate annotation variation patterns in graded human ratings for inappropriate languages, including offensive language, hate speech, and toxic language perception. We examine whether the degree of annotation disagreement can be predicted from textual features. We further propose the Opposition Index, a metric that quantifies perspective opposition among annotators on a given item, and investigate the predictability of instances with potentially opposing human opinions. Our results show a moderate positive correlation between estimated and observed annotation variance. We find that two approaches achieve comparable performance in variance prediction: directly predicting the variance value and estimating it from predicted annotation distributions. Our results on opposition perspective prediction show that items with high opposition index values are more difficult to predict and are often underestimated by models.

</details>


### [72] [Compute Optimal Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01188)
*Tomasz Limisiewicz,Artidoro Pagnoni,Srini Iyer,Mike Lewis,Sachin Mehta,Alisa Liu,Margaret Li,Gargi Ghosh,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了token的压缩率（即每个token对应的平均字节数）对语言模型扩展规律的影响，发现计算最优配置下模型参数量应与数据的字节数成比例，而非传统的token数，并指出最优压缩率随计算量增加而降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管缩放定律被广泛用于选择数据量和模型大小，但作为数据单位的token的信息粒度如何影响这一关系尚不清楚。本文旨在系统性地探究token压缩率在其中的作用。

Method: 训练了988个具有不同压缩率的50M到7B参数的潜在token化模型（BLT），从而能够灵活控制并研究超出传统BPE分词器（4.57字节/Token）范围的压缩率对扩展趋势的影响。

Result: 发现在计算最优配置下，模型参数量与以字节为单位的数据量成正比，而不是以token为单位；同时发现最优压缩率低于传统BPE结果，且随计算量增加而下降；该结论在潜在分词和子词分词以及非英语语言中均具有一致性。

Conclusion: token的压缩率显著影响模型的扩展行为，建议在设计语言模型时根据计算预算选择更优的压缩率和分词方案，以实现更高的计算效率。

Abstract: Scaling laws enable the optimal selection of data amount and language model size, yet the impact of the data unit, the token, on this relationship remains underexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate how the information granularity of tokens, controlled by the compression rate (i.e., average bytes of text per token), affects scaling trends. We train 988 latent tokenized models (BLT) ranging from 50M to 7B parameters that enable setting the desired compression rate. This flexibility allows us to study the role of compression rate well beyond 4.57 bytes per token obtained with a popular BPE tokenizer. Our experiments reveal that in compute-optimal configurations, model parameter counts scale proportionally to data size measured in bytes, not in tokens as commonly perceived (Kaplan et al., 2020; Hoffmann et al., 2022). Furthermore, we discover that the optimal compression rate differs from the one obtained with BPE and decreases with compute. These findings generalize to both latent and subword tokenization, as well as to languages other than English, guiding language model developers on tokenization scheme selection for maximal compute efficiency.

</details>


### [73] [SRA: Span Representation Alignment for Large Language Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01205)
*Quoc Phong Dao,Hoang Son Nguyen,Pham Khanh Chi,Tung Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Nguyen Thi Ngoc Diep,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SRA（Span Representation Alignment），一种基于多粒子动力系统视角的跨分词器知识蒸馏框架，通过将对齐单元从脆弱的token提升为鲁棒的span，并利用注意力加权的质心表示和几何正则化来增强跨模型知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨分词器知识蒸馏方法依赖于token级对齐，易受分词器差异影响，缺乏鲁棒性。本文旨在探索更稳定的语义单元（如span）进行对齐，以提升蒸馏效果。

Method: 将每个span建模为粒子集群，用注意力权重计算其质心（CoM）作为语义表示；引入几何正则项保持表示空间结构，并设计对齐的span logit蒸馏策略以促进知识转移。整体框架从物理系统的角度重新定义跨分词器蒸馏。

Result: 在具有挑战性的跨架构蒸馏实验中，SRA显著优于当前最先进的CTKD基线方法。

Conclusion: SRA通过引入span级质心对齐与物理启发的建模方式，有效提升了跨分词器知识蒸馏的鲁棒性与性能，验证了从更高层次语义单元进行对齐的优势。

Abstract: Cross-Tokenizer Knowledge Distillation (CTKD) enables knowledge transfer between a large language model and a smaller student, even when they employ different tokenizers. While existing approaches mainly focus on token-level alignment strategies, which are often brittle and sensitive to discrepancies between tokenizers, we argue that the method of aggregating tokens into more robust representations before distillation is of equal importance. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{SRA} (\textbf{S}pan \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}lignment for Large Language Model Distillation), a novel framework that reframes CTKD through the physical lens of Multi-Particle Dynamical Systems. SRA shifts the fundamental unit of alignment from tokens to robust, tokenizer-agnostic spans. We model each span as a cluster of particles and represent its state by its Center of Mass (CoM) - an attention-weighted average that captures rich semantic information. We leverage the concept of span centers of mass with attention-derived weighting to prioritize the most salient spans. In addition, we employ a geometric regularizer to preserve the structural integrity of the representation space and introduce aligned span logit distillation to enhance knowledge transfer across models. In challenging cross-architecture distillation experiments, SRA consistently and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art CTKD baselines, validating our physically-grounded approach.

</details>


### [74] [Lost in the Tower of Babel: The Adverse Effects of Incidental Multilingualism in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01224)
*Anjishnu Mukherjee,Chutong Meng,Antonios Anastasopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批评了当前多语言NLP中偶然性多语言主义的脆弱范式，主张转向“设计上的多语言主义”，将公平的多语言表现、文化根基和跨语言行为理解作为模型 pipeline 的首要目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为当前大语言模型看似多语言的原因是训练数据的庞杂而非对多语言能力的主动设计，这种偶然性导致了语言间表现不均、系统脆弱且行为不透明，尤其在需要跨语言推理与行动的实际应用中问题严重。

Method: 通过一项聚焦实证研究探讨两个问题：模型自我报告支持的语言 vs 实际响应的语言；并展示简单的语言切换攻击如何暴露模型在多语言处理中的失败与隐藏假设。

Result: 发现模型在不同语言上的行为存在显著差异，自我宣称的语言支持与实际响应不符，且简单的语言变化即可触发错误，揭示出系统对语言处理的深层缺陷。

Conclusion: 呼吁建立“设计上的多语言主义”研究议程，将多语言能力和文化敏感性从训练到部署全过程纳入核心设计目标，以实现真正稳健和公平的多语言NLP系统。

Abstract: This paper argues that contemporary multilingual NLP has converged on a fragile and misleading paradigm of incidental multilingualism. Today's LLMs appear multilingual largely because they are trained on massive, uneven web corpora, not because multilingual or multicultural competence has been treated as a core design objective.
