*Huiqian Lai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLM）在评估新闻可信度时表现出对娱乐新闻与严肃新闻的不平等判断，存在显著且模型特定的假阳性差异，提示需进行基于新闻类型的分层评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探讨零样本下的大型语言模型是否在不同新闻类型（如娱乐新闻与严肃新闻）中采用一致的可信度判断标准，揭示潜在的系统性偏见。

Method: 使用FakeNewsNet中的GossipCop数据集，采用内部对照设计，测试四种前沿LLM；通过风格交换实验和提示词缓解策略分析风格与提示的影响，并结合定性编码识别错误模式。

Result: DeepSeek-V3.2和GPT-5.2在娱乐新闻上假阳性率分别高出10.1和8.8个百分点（p<0.001），而Claude Opus 4.6和Gemini 3 Flash无显著差异；风格交换效果有限，特定提示可使DeepSeek-V3.2假阳性下降50%但不具通用性；定性分析发现两类常见误判：将私人生活声明视为不可验证、贬低娱乐新闻的认知合法性。

Conclusion: LLM在新闻可信度评估中可能不仅判断事实真假，还隐含对不同新闻体裁合法性的差异化承认；总体准确率可能掩盖结构性偏差，因此评估应包含按新闻类型分层的假阳性分析。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated news credibility assessment, yet it remains unclear whether they apply even-handed standards across journalistic genres. We examine whether zero-shot LLMs are more likely to misclassify legitimate entertainment news as fake than legitimate hard news, using a within-dataset design on GossipCop from FakeNewsNet. Across four frontier models, we find a clear but model-specific genre asymmetry: DeepSeek-V3.2 and GPT-5.2 show false-positive-rate gaps of 10.1 and 8.8 percentage points, respectively (both $p < .001$), whereas Claude Opus 4.6 and Gemini 3 Flash show no comparable difference. A style-swap experiment yields only limited and inconsistent changes, suggesting that the asymmetry is not reducible to stylistic register alone. Prompt-based mitigation is likewise possible but not generic: framing the model as an entertainment-news fact-checker reduces false positives for DeepSeek-V3.2 by about 50\% without detectable recall loss, but offers little improvement for GPT-5.2. Exploratory qualitative coding further suggests two recurring error patterns in sampled false positives: treating private-life claims as inherently unverifiable and discounting entertainment journalism as an epistemically weaker genre. Taken together, these findings show that aggregate performance metrics can obscure structured false positives within legitimate journalism. We argue that LLM-based credibility assessment may not only evaluate truth claims but also differentially recognize the legitimacy of journalistic genres, and that evaluation should therefore include genre-stratified false-positive analysis alongside overall accuracy.

</details>


### [151] [Catching the Infection Before It Spreads: Foresight-Guided Defense in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01758)
*Yue Ma,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的前瞻性引导局部净化（FLP）框架，通过模拟未来行为轨迹和多角色预测来检测并清除多智能体系统中的传染性越狱攻击，有效降低感染率并保持交互多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法依赖全局共享的“治愈因子”，难以应对源于局部交互的传染性越狱攻击，且会削弱智能体响应的多样性，导致防御表面化。

Method: 每个智能体通过推理未来交互行为轨迹进行自我诊断；引入多角色模拟策略以增强预测鲁棒性；利用检索结果与语义层面的响应多样性作为感染检测信号；对感染者采用局部净化：近期感染通过相册回滚处理，长期感染通过递归二分诊断（RBD）定位并清除病毒样本。

Result: 实验显示，FLP将最大累积感染率从超过95%降至5.47%以下，且检索与语义指标接近正常基线，表明其能有效抑制感染并保留交互多样性。

Conclusion: FLP通过局部化、前瞻性的行为建模，克服了传统全局防御与局部威胁之间的不匹配问题，实现了高效且多样性的多智能体系统安全防护。

Abstract: Large multimodal model-based Multi-Agent Systems (MASs) enable collaborative complex problem solving through specialized agents. However, MASs are vulnerable to infectious jailbreak, where compromising a single agent can spread to others, leading to widespread compromise. Existing defenses counter this by training a more contagious cure factor, biasing agents to retrieve it over virus adversarial examples (VirAEs). However, this homogenizes agent responses, providing only superficial suppression rather than true recovery. We revisit these defenses, which operate globally via a shared cure factor, while infectious jailbreak arise from localized interaction behaviors. This mismatch limits their effectiveness. To address this, we propose a training-free Foresight-Guided Local Purification (FLP) framework, where each agent reasons over future interactions to track behavioral evolution and eliminate infections. Specifically, each agent simulates future behavioral trajectories over subsequent chat rounds. To reflect diversity in MASs, we introduce a multi-persona simulation strategy for robust prediction across interaction contexts. We then use response diversity as a diagnostic signal to detect infection by analyzing inconsistencies across persona-based predictions at both retrieval-result and semantic levels. For infected agents, we apply localized purification: recent infections are mitigated via immediate album rollback, while long-term infections are handled using Recursive Binary Diagnosis (RBD), which recursively partitions the image album and applies the same diagnosis strategy to localize and eliminate VirAEs. Experiments show that FLP reduces the maximum cumulative infection rate from over 95% to below 5.47%. Moreover, retrieval and semantic metrics closely match benign baselines, indicating effective preservation of interaction diversity.

</details>


### [152] [DataEvolver: Let Your Data Build and Improve Itself via Goal-Driven Loop Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01789)
*Qisong Zhang,Wenzhuo Wu,Zhuangzhuang Jia,Yunhao Yang,Huayu Zhang,Xianghao Zang,Zhixiang He,Zhongjiang He,Kongming Liang,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataEvolver是一个闭环视觉数据引擎，通过目标驱动的生成、校正与验证循环，提升可控视觉数据的构建效率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可控的视觉数据在图像编辑和多模态理解中面临瓶颈，传统单次渲染难以提供足够监督信号，需要迭代式的生成与修正过程。

Method: 提出DataEvolver，采用双循环架构：生成时的自我校正（样本级）和验证时的自我扩展（数据集轮次级），支持多种视觉工件类型，并引入明确目标、持久化 artifact、有限纠正动作和接受决策机制。

Result: 在对象旋转图像编辑任务中，使用固定Qwen-Edit LoRA探针，Ours+DualGate模型在SpatialEdit和保留测试集上优于未适配基线和公开多角度LoRA；消融实验显示从场景感知生成到反馈驱动校正再到双门控验证的持续改进路径。

Conclusion: DataEvolver不仅生成了高质量旋转数据，更贡献了一个可复用的视觉数据构建框架，强调目标追踪、评审、修正与接受的闭环机制。

Abstract: Constructing controllable visual data is a major bottleneck for image editing and multimodal understanding. Useful supervision is rarely produced by a single rendering pass; instead it emerges through iterative generation, inspection, correction, filtering, and export. We present DataEvolver, a closed-loop visual data engine that organizes this process around explicit goals, persistent artifacts, bounded corrective actions, and acceptance decisions. DataEvolver supports multiple artifact types, including RGB images, masks, depth maps, normal maps, meshes, poses, trajectories, and review traces. In the current release, the system operates through two coupled loops: generation-time self-correction within each sample and validation-time self-expansion across dataset rounds. We validate the framework on an image-level object-rotation setting. With a fixed Qwen-Edit LoRA probe, our final Ours+DualGate model outperforms both the unadapted base model and a public multi-angle LoRA on SpatialEdit and a held-out evaluation set. Ablations show a consistent improvement path from scene-aware generation to feedback-driven correction and dual-gated validation. Beyond the released rotation data, our main contribution is a reusable framework for building visual datasets through explicit goal tracking, review, correction, and acceptance loops.

</details>


### [153] [NeuroState-Bench: A Human-Calibrated Benchmark for Commitment Integrity in LLM Agent Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01847)
*Jia Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuroState-Bench是一个人类校准的基准，通过明确定义的侧查询探针来评估多轮任务中智能体承诺的一致性，揭示任务成功与承诺完整性之间的差异，并提供更稳定的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仅基于结果的评估无法准确判断智能体在多轮任务中是否保持了必要的承诺一致性，因此需要一种更精细、可解释的评估方式来揭示潜在的认知偏差和状态漂移问题。

Method: 构建了一个包含144个确定性任务和306个侧查询探针的基准测试集，覆盖八类认知动机驱动的失败模式；采用人类校准的标注流程，并设计HCCIS-CORE等评分模型来量化承诺完整性，比较不同模型在任务成功与承诺一致性上的表现差异。

Result: 实验显示任务成功率与承诺完整性显著不一致：32个智能体中有31个在两种指标下排名变化；完整性排名对干扰更具鲁棒性；HCCIS-CORE在AUC和PR-AUC上表现最优，且更贴近基准设计意图；神经增强变体表现较弱，随机控制接近随机水平。

Conclusion: NeuroState-Bench提供了一个经过人类校准的新评估维度，能够有效暴露更大范围内模型在多轮交互中的承诺失败问题，推动超越单纯结果导向的智能体评估标准。

Abstract: Outcome-only evaluation under-specifies whether an evaluated agent profile preserves the commitments required to solve a multi-turn task coherently. NeuroState-Bench is a human-calibrated benchmark that operationalizes commitment integrity through benchmark-defined side-query probes rather than inferred hidden activations. The released inventory contains 144 deterministic tasks and 306 benchmark-defined side-query probes spanning eight cognitively motivated failure families, paired clean and distractor variants, and three difficulty bands. The main 32-profile evaluation contains a fixed 16-profile local subset and a matched 16-profile hosted large-model subset evaluated through the same benchmark pipeline. Human calibration uses the final merged reporting scope: 104 sampled task units, 216 raw annotations, and 108 adjudicated task rows, with weighted kappa = 0.977 and ICC(2,1) = 0.977. Empirically, task success and commitment integrity diverge across this expanded grid: the success leader is not the integrity leader, 31 of 32 profiles change rank when integrity replaces task success, and integrity rankings are more stable under distractor perturbation. The primary confidence-free score HCCIS-CORE reaches 0.8469 AUC and 0.6992 PR-AUC for post-probe diagnostic discrimination of terminal task failure; the legacy full heuristic variant HCCIS-FULL reaches 0.7997 AUC and 0.6410 PR-AUC. Probe accuracy and state drift achieve slightly higher ROC-AUC, 0.8587, and better Brier/ECE, while HCCIS-CORE has substantially higher point-estimate PR-AUC and remains more closely tied to the benchmark's intended construct. The exploratory neural-augmented variant HCCIS+N is weaker overall, and a randomized subspace control approaches chance. NeuroState-Bench therefore contributes a calibrated evaluation axis for exposing commitment failures over a broader model grid than the original local-only subset.

</details>


### [154] [Sheaf-Theoretic Planning: A Categorical Foundation for Resilient Multi-Agent Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01879)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于层论与拓扑斯理论的多智能体规划新范式——层论规划（STP），以克服传统逻辑系统在开放、动态环境中因闭世界假设导致的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体系统依赖于闭世界假设的符号逻辑模型，在面对未观测干预、计划中断和信念与现实偏差时表现脆弱，难以适应复杂现实环境。

Method: 引入层论与拓扑斯理论作为数学基础，利用层的局部一致性和全局粘合机制来建模多智能体环境中的动作、状态变迁与知识共享，提供对动态变化和异构信念的自然表达。

Result: 建立了STP的范畴论基础，证明其在处理开放世界推理、分布式感知融合和计划鲁棒性方面优于传统方法，并探讨了其实现可行性和扩展方向。

Conclusion: STP为构建具备弹性与适应性的自主智能体系统提供了坚实的理论框架，代表了从经典逻辑向结构化、几何化知识表示的重要范式转变。

Abstract: The challenge of engineering autonomous agents capable of navigating the stochastic and adversarial nature of the physical world has historically resided at the intersection of symbolic logic and control theory. Traditional multi-agent system (MAS) frameworks have relied heavily on monolithic logical models -- primarily variations of the event calculus and situation calculus -- to represent action, change, and temporal persistence. While these classical systems provide robust solutions to the frame problem through mechanisms like circumscription and successor state axioms, they are inherently limited by a closed-world assumption that fails in the face of unobserved agent interventions, plan interruptions, and divergent belief-reality states. The paradigm of Sheaf-Theoretic Planning (STP) emerges as a transformative alternative, grounding the problem of multi-agent coordination under the mathematical structures of topos theory and sheaf semantics. This report provides an exhaustive analysis, justification, and extension of the STP framework, exploring its categorical foundations, implementation feasibility, and role in the future of resilient autonomous systems.

</details>


### [155] [Disentangling Intent from Role: Adversarial Self-Play for Persona-Invariant Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01899)
*Jiajia Li,Xiaoyu Wen,Zhongtian Ma,Shuyue Hu,Qiaosheng Zhang,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Persona-Invariant Alignment (PIA)的对抗自博弈框架，用于防御基于角色的越狱攻击，通过攻击端的角色谱系演化（PLE）和防御端的角色不变性一致性学习（PICL）实现协同进化，并在理论上和实验上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型尽管在安全对齐方面有所进展，但仍易受新兴的基于角色的越狱攻击影响，且当前研究缺乏对防御机制的系统性和机理性约束。

Method: 提出PIA框架，包含攻击侧的Persona Lineage Evolution (PLE) 和防御侧的 Persona-Invariant Consistency Learning (PICL)；理论基础上采用单向KL散度约束，实现安全决策与角色上下文的结构解耦。

Result: 实验表明PLE能有效探索高风险角色空间，PICL显著降低了攻击成功率（ASR），同时保持模型通用能力。

Conclusion: PIA提供了一种鲁棒且优越的对齐范式，能够在抵御基于角色的越狱攻击的同时维持模型的安全性和功能性。

Abstract: The growing capabilities of large language models (LLMs) have driven their widespread deployment across diverse domains, even in potentially high-risk scenarios. Despite advances in safety alignment techniques, current models remain vulnerable to emerging persona-based jailbreak attacks. Existing research on persona-based jailbreak has primarily focused on attack iterations, yet it lacks systemic and mechanistic constraints on the defense side. To address this challenge, we propose Persona-Invariant Alignment (PIA), an adversarial self-play framework that achieves co-evolution through Persona Lineage Evolution (PLE) on the attack side and Persona-Invariant Consistency Learning (PICL) on the defense side. Theoretically, PICL is grounded in the structural separation hypothesis, using a unilateral KL-divergence constraint to enable the structural decoupling of safety decisions from persona context, thereby maintaining safe behavior under persona-based jailbreak attacks. Experimental results demonstrate that PLE efficiently explores high-risk persona spaces by leveraging lineage-based credit propagation. Meanwhile, the PICL defense method significantly reduces the Attack Success Rate (ASR) while preserving the model's general capability, thereby validating the superiority and robustness of this alignment paradigm. Codes are available at https://github.com/JiajiaLi-1130/PIA.

</details>


### [156] [Moira: Language-driven Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Pair Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01954)
*Polydoros Giannouris,Yuechen Jiang,Lingfei Qian,Yuyan Wang,Xueqing Peng,Jimin Huang,Guojun Xiong,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种语言驱动的分层强化学习框架，使用大语言模型作为分层策略，通过提示更新来优化配对交易中的抽象选择与执行，实现在延迟反馈下的有效学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有分层结构的序贯决策问题中由于信用分配困难导致的学习挑战，特别是在抽象错误、执行不佳或两者交互影响下的性能下降问题。

Method: 将配对交易建模为分层强化学习问题，高层和低层策略均由大语言模型参数化，并仅通过提示更新进行优化；利用轨迹级和回合级文本反馈调整抽象与执行，显式分离抽象选择与执行以减少非平稳性。

Result: 在真实市场数据上的实验表明，该方法在传统及基于LLM的基线之上实现了持续改进，验证了语言驱动分层强化学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 语言驱动的提示优化框架能够有效支持分层决策任务中的信用分配，适用于长周期语义推理与短周期部分可观执行相结合的复杂环境。

Abstract: Many sequential decision-making problems exhibit hierarchical structure, where high-level semantic choices constrain downstream actions and feedback is delayed and ambiguous. Learning in such settings is challenging due to credit assignment: performance degradation may arise from flawed abstractions, suboptimal execution, or their interaction. We study this challenge through pair trading, a domain that naturally combines long-horizon semantic reasoning for asset pair selection with short-horizon execution under partial observability. We formulate pair trading as a hierarchical reinforcement learning problem and propose a language-driven optimization framework in which both high-level and low-level policies are parameterized by large language models (LLMs) and optimized exclusively through prompt updates. Our approach leverages pretrained LLMs as hierarchical policies and uses trajectory- and episode-level textual feedback to adapt abstractions and execution without gradient-based fine-tuning. By explicitly separating abstraction selection from execution, the framework reduces non-stationarity across hierarchical levels and enables targeted adaptation under delayed feedback. Experiments on real-world market data show consistent improvements over traditional and LLM-based baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of language-driven hierarchical reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [157] [Personalized Digital Health Modeling with Adaptive Support Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02004)
*Zhongqi Yang,Mahkameh Rasouli,Neda Mohseni,Yong Huang,Iman Azimi,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的个性化建模框架，通过自适应加权相似与不相似用户数据，提升数字健康中稀缺数据下的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于个体间生理和行为差异大，且用户特异性数据稀少噪声多，现有方法在迁移学习时易产生偏差，泛化能力弱。

Method: 设计了一个联合优化目标，结合个人损失、基于相似性的加权迁移以及来自不相似用户的对比正则化，并通过迭代算法联合更新模型参数和用户权重。

Result: 在四个真实世界数字健康数据集的六项任务上，显著优于群体和个性化基线模型，在大规模数据上RMSE降低最多达10%，在低数据场景下约降低25%。

Conclusion: 该框架提高了数据利用效率，增强了模型泛化能力，并提供可解释的用户权重用于指导数据选择。

Abstract: Personalized models are essential in digital health because individuals exhibit substantial physiological and behavioral heterogeneity. Yet personalization is limited by scarce and noisy user-specific data. Most existing methods rely on population pretraining or data from similar users only, which can lead to biased transfer and weak generalization. We propose a unified personalization framework that trains a personal model using adaptively weighted support users, including both similar and dissimilar individuals. The objective integrates personal loss, similarity-weighted transfer from similar users, and contrastive regularization from dissimilar users to suppress misleading correlations. An iterative optimization algorithm jointly updates model parameters and user similarity weights. Experiments on six tasks across four real-world digital health datasets show consistent improvements over population and personalized baselines. The method achieves up to 10% lower RMSE on large-scale datasets and approximately 25% lower RMSE in low-data settings. The learned adaptive weights improve data efficiency and provide interpretable guidance for targeted data selection.

</details>


### [158] [Reliable AI Needs to Externalize Implicit Knowledge: A Human-AI Collaboration Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02010)
*Hengyu Liu,Tianyi Li,Zhihong Cui,Yushuai Li,Zhangkai Wu,Torben Bach Pedersen,Kristian Torp,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出知识对象（KOs）以解决人工智能中隐性知识验证的难题，提升AI可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于文档化成本过高，隐性知识未被充分外化，但AI却从中学习，导致可能获取有害偏见，现有方法难以验证这些隐性知识。

Method: 提出“知识对象”（Knowledge Objects, KOs）作为结构化工具，将隐性知识显性化，使其可被人类审查、验证和认可。

Result: KOs 能够改变验证的成本效益，使原本难以验证的知识变得可验证，并通过积累的人类验证提升AI系统的长期可靠性。

Conclusion: 可靠的人工智能需要基础设施来支持对隐性知识的人类验证，知识对象为填补当前可靠性方法的根本空白提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This position paper argues that reliable AI requires infrastructure for human validation of implicit knowledge. AI learns from both explicit knowledge (papers, documentation, structured databases) and implicit knowledge (reasoning patterns, debugging processes, intermediate steps). Implicit knowledge remains unexternalized because documentation cost exceeds perceived value -- yet AI learns from it indiscriminately, acquiring both beneficial patterns and harmful biases. Current reliability methods can only verify explicit knowledge against sources, creating a fundamental gap: the most valuable AI capabilities (reasoning, judgment, intuition) are precisely those we cannot verify. We propose Knowledge Objects (KOs) -- structured artifacts that externalize implicit knowledge into forms humans can inspect, verify, and endorse. KOs transform verification economics: what was previously too costly to verify becomes feasible, enabling accumulated human validation to improve reliability over time.

</details>


### [159] [Tenability and Weak Semantics: Modeling Non-uniform Defense -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02024)
*Uri Andrews,Luca San Mauro,John Spoerl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了可维持性（tenability）这一基于对话的语义家族，用于形式化在辩论中支持者能否针对对手提出的任何无冲突攻击维护指定论点，并定义了三种变体，分析了其相对强度、与已有弱语义的关系及计算复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的弱语义虽然放宽了可接受性的要求，但仍对合理但集体不一致的论点要求统一防御，这在防御具有策略性时可能过于严格。因此需要一种更灵活的语义来处理这种情形。

Method: 通过在有限无冲突移动上的单调承诺博弈，定义了三种可维持性变体：静态可维持性、可维持性和强可维持性，并比较了它们之间的强度关系，证明了与先前研究的弱语义之间的蕴含和分离结果。

Result: 可维持性语义在三个自然基准模式上表现出与以往弱语义不同的行为；静态可维持性的判定是$Π^P_2$-完全的，而可维持性和强可维持性的判定是PSPACE-完全的。

Conclusion: 可维持性提供了一种新的基于对话的框架来评估论点的可接受性，在策略性防御场景下比传统弱语义更具表达力和灵活性。

Abstract: In Dung-style abstract argumentation, various semantics capture notions of acceptability of arguments. The admissibility semantics capture the notion that an argument can be consistently defended from any potential counterargument. Weak semantics often relax the demands of admissibility by restricting which counterarguments must be taken seriously (e.g., discounting self-defeating or otherwise incoherent attacks). Many prominent proposals for weak semantics remain extension-based in a stronger sense. While these semantics discount attacks from arguments which are considered unreasonable, they still require a uniform defense against all reasonable arguments, even if they are collectively inconsistent. This uniformity can be too demanding when defensibility is inherently strategic, and thus the appropriate reply depends on the opponent's line of attack.
  We introduce tenability, a family of dialogue-based semantics that formalize when a designated argument (or a set of arguments) can be maintained in debate by a proponent against any conflict-free attack which the opponent may present. The approach is motivated by three natural benchmark patterns: self-defeating attack, floating assignment, and disjunctive reinstatement, on which tenability behaves differently from all weak semantics previously considered in the literature. We define three variants -- static tenability, tenability, and strong tenability -- via monotone commitment games over finite conflict-free moves, differing in the obligations imposed on the disputants. We establish the relative strength of these notions, prove implications and separations with previously studied weak semantics, and we analyze computational complexity on finite frameworks: deciding static tenability is $Π^P_2$-complete, while deciding tenability and strong tenability is PSPACE-complete.

</details>


### [160] [NORA: A Harness-Engineered Autonomous Research Agent for End-to-End Spatial Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02092)
*Bing Zhou,Xiao Huang,Huan Ning,Qiusheng Wu,Diya Li,Ziyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NORA——一个专为地理信息科学（GIScience）和空间数据科学设计的多智能体自主研究系统，通过领域定制化的“技能优先”架构和“引导工程”方法，显著提升了科研自动化中的效率与研究质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主科研智能体大多缺乏针对空间数据科学所需的专门推理、方法选择和数据获取能力，难以满足该领域严谨研究的需求，因此需要构建领域专用的智能科研系统。

Method: 提出并实现了NORA系统，采用“技能优先”架构，包含21种领域专用技能、9个专业子智能体及自定义MCP服务器；引入两种新型空间分析技能：空间分析技能单元和空间数据下载技能；并形式化了“引导工程”概念，集成生命周期钩子、安全门控、生成-评估分离、人机协同和状态持久化机制。

Result: 通过对6位领域专家和3位LLM评审者的案例研究，在新颖性、质量、严谨性等七个维度上评估表明，相比通用智能体配置，领域定制化的引导工程显著提高了研究输出的效率和质量。

Conclusion: 领域专用的架构设计与引导工程是实现高质量、可重复、可靠的自主科研的关键，NORA为未来学科定制型AI科研助手提供了可行范式。

Abstract: The automation of scientific research workflows has emerged as a transformative frontier in artificial intelligence, yet existing autonomous research agents remain largely domain-agnostic, lacking the specialized reasoning, method selection, and data acquisition capabilities required for rigorous spatial data science. This paper introduces NORA (Night Owl Research Agent), a harness-engineered, multi-agent autonomous research system purpose-built for GIScience and spatial data science. NORA orchestrates the complete research lifecycle through a skills-first architecture comprising 21 domain-specialized workflow skills, 9 specialist sub-agents, and custom Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers. Central to the system's design are two novel domain-specialized skills: a spatial analysis skill unit that encodes decision frameworks for exploratory spatial data analysis, spatial regression, and diagnostics; and a spatial data download skill that supports reproducible acquisition from authoritative geospatial data sources. We formalize the concept of harness engineering for scientific research agents, demonstrating how lifecycle hooks, safety gates, generator-evaluator separation, human-in-the-loop, and state persistence ensure reliable and reproducible autonomous research. We evaluate NORA through case studies by 6 domain specialists and 3 LLM reviewers across seven dimensions (novelty, quality, rigor, etc). Results demonstrate that domain-specialized harness engineering substantially improves the efficiency and quality of research output compared to general-purpose agent configurations.

</details>


### [161] [The Dynamic Gist-Based Memory Model (DGMM): A Memory-Centric Architecture for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02106)
*Terry Dorsey,Kevin Huggins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以记忆为中心的人工智能架构——动态要点记忆模型（DGMM），将经验显式、持久地表示为时序化、图结构的 episodic-semantic 记忆，支持可解释性、时间锚定和来源追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型将经验隐式编码于固定参数中，导致记忆不可持续、难以追溯和解释，缺乏时间连续性和上下文感知能力。

Method: 提出DGMM架构，采用图结构的显式记忆存储经验，通过基于线索的条件召回机制构建工作记忆，并定义增量式记忆增长与召回驱动解释的架构不变性。

Result: DGMM实现了经验的持久化存储、基于上下文的动态检索、局部惊讶响应以及无需修改存储结构的情境可变性。

Conclusion: DGMM为人工智能提供了一个可解释、可追溯、时间连续且无需重新训练即可演进理解的记忆基础架构。

Abstract: Contemporary artificial intelligence systems achieve strong performance through large-scale parameterization, retrieval augmentation, and training on extensive static corpora. Despite these advances, they continue to face limitations in persistent memory, temporal grounding, provenance, and interpretability. These challenges are especially pronounced in large language models, where experience is encoded implicitly in fixed parameters, limiting the ability to preserve, inspect, and reinterpret past interactions over time.
  This paper establishes a memory-centric architectural foundation for artificial intelligence in which experience is represented explicitly and persistently to support temporal grounding, provenance, and interpretability. It proposes an alternative to parameter-centric approaches by treating memory as a first-class, structured substrate for reasoning.
  We introduce the Dynamic Gist-Based Memory Model (DGMM), an architecture in which experience is represented as an evolving, graph-structured episodic-semantic memory. DGMM encodes experience as interconnected conceptual structures grounded in time, source, and interaction context, and defines selective, cue-conditioned recall as the mechanism for constructing working memory. A formal schema and architectural invariants are provided based on additive memory growth and recall-conditioned interpretation.
  The results specify properties of DGMM, including episodic persistence, locality of cue-conditioned surprise, and contextual variability without structural modification of stored memory. DGMM provides a coherent architectural theory in which memory is explicit and persistent, supporting evolving interpretation without retraining and enabling interpretable, context-aware, and temporally grounded AI systems.

</details>


### [162] [Planner Matters! An Efficient and Unbalanced Multi-agent Collaboration Framework for Long-horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02168)
*Wenyi Wu,Sibo Zhu,Kun Zhou,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种以规划器为中心的多智能体框架，通过将计算资源集中于高层规划来提升长视野任务的性能，实验证明该方法在多个基准上具有高效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体在长视野规划和推理任务中表现仍有限，需改进其架构与计算资源分配。

Method: 设计包含规划者、执行者和记忆管理者的三角色多智能体框架，并通过系统性的计算分配分析，提出仅优化规划者的强化学习方法，使用基于VLM的评判器提供轨迹级奖励。

Result: 实验表明规划是影响性能的主要因素，执行和记忆模块所需计算较少；在网页导航、操作系统控制和工具使用等任务中，该方法实现了计算高效且性能优越的自动化。

Conclusion: 将模型容量和学习重点集中在高层规划上，能显著提升长视野任务的效率与效果，验证了规划在智能体系统中的核心作用。

Abstract: Language model (LM)-based agents have demonstrated promising capabilities in automating complex tasks from natural language instructions, yet they continue to struggle with long-horizon planning and reasoning. To address this, we propose an enhanced multi-agent framework that decomposes automation into three roles: a planner for high-level decision-making, an actor for task execution, and a memory manager for contextual reasoning. While this modular decomposition aligns with established design patterns, our core contribution lies in a systematic compute-allocation analysis, revealing that planning is the dominant factor influencing task performance. Execution and memory management require significantly less compute and model capacity to achieve competitive results. Building on these insights, we introduce a planner-centric reinforcement learning approach, which exclusively optimizes the planner using trajectory-level rewards from a VLM-as-judge, while freezing the other components. Extensive experiments on benchmarks spanning web navigation, OS control, and tool use demonstrate that concentrating model capacity and learning on high-level planning yields robust and compute-efficient improvements in long-horizon agent automation. Our code is publicly released.

</details>


### [163] [Retrieval and Multi-Hop Reasoning in 1M-Token Context Windows: Evaluating LLMs on Classical Chinese Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02173)
*Eric H. C. Chow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了五种具有100万token上下文窗口的前沿大语言模型在古典中文语料上的长上下文检索与推理能力。单针检索任务显示最强模型（Gemini、Claude、GPT-5.5）表现接近完美，但在三跳链式推理任务中表现出三种不同的性能衰减模式，揭示了1M上下文长度宣称并不能真实反映多跳推理能力，512K到1M的过渡阶段是区分模型能力的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验大模型在1M token上下文下是否真正具备长距离信息检索和复杂推理能力，而非依赖训练数据记忆，并探索上下文长度与实际可用能力之间的关系。

Method: 设计两项实验：一是1M token输入下的单针检索测试，包含真实与篡改的传记信息以区分上下文检索与记忆；二是三跳链式推理任务，评估模型在256K、512K和1M上下文中的多步推理能力。

Result: 单针检索中Gemini 3.1 Pro、Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5.5达到100%准确率；多跳推理中发现三种衰减模式：稳定型（Gemini、Claude）、晚期骤降型（GPT-5.5、Qwen3.6-plus）和渐进衰退型（DeepSeek V4 Pro）。

Conclusion: 名义上的上下文长度不能代表实际多跳推理能力，512K到1M的过渡表现是当前顶级长上下文模型的核心差异点。

Abstract: We evaluate the long-context retrieval and reasoning capabilities of five frontier large language models with advertised 1M-token context windows on a classical Chinese corpus. Two complementary studies are reported. Test 1 measures single-needle retrieval at 1M tokens of input, with three biographical needles planted at three depths and pairs of real (training-prior-consistent) and altered (training-prior-contradicting) variants to separate genuine in-context retrieval from reliance on memorised training data. Test 2, a follow-up designed to probe whether long-context capability degrades when retrieval requires intermediate reasoning, measures three-hop chain traversal across three context tiers (256K, 512K, and 1M tokens). We find that single-needle retrieval at 1M is essentially solved for the strongest models - Gemini 3.1 Pro, Claude Opus 4.7, and GPT-5.5 each achieve 100% - but that multi-hop performance reveals three distinct decay signatures: a stable regime (Gemini Pro, Claude) maintaining greater than 80% accuracy through 512K with modest degradation at 1M; a late-cliff regime (GPT-5.5, Qwen3.6-plus) collapsing sharply between 512K and 1M; and a smooth-decline regime (DeepSeek V4 Pro) decaying gradually across the entire range. The findings suggest that nominal context-window length is a poor proxy for usable long-context multi-hop capability, and that the sharpest discriminator between current 1M-context flagships is the 512K-to-1M transition.

</details>


### [164] [T$^2$PO: Uncertainty-Guided Exploration Control for Stable Multi-Turn Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02178)
*Haixin Wang,Hejie Cui,Chenwei Zhang,Xin Liu,Shuowei Jin,Shijie Geng,Xinyang Zhang,Nasser Zalmout,Zhenyu Shi,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为T²PO的不确定性感知框架，通过在token和turn级别上精细控制探索来提高多轮强化学习中的训练稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多轮强化学习方法存在由于低效探索导致的训练不稳定问题，尤其是在策略持续产生信息量低的动作时。

Method: T²PO在token级别监控不确定性动态，并在边际不确定性变化低于阈值时触发思考干预；在turn级别识别探索进展可忽略的交互并动态重采样以避免浪费的 rollout。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和Search QA等多个环境中评估，T²PO显著提升了训练稳定性和性能，同时提高了探索效率。

Conclusion: T²PO通过细粒度的探索控制有效缓解了多轮强化学习中的训练崩溃问题，展现出更强的稳定性和更高的任务完成效率。

Abstract: Recent progress in multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) has significantly improved reasoning LLMs' performances on complex interactive tasks. Despite advances in stabilization techniques such as fine-grained credit assignment and trajectory filtering, instability remains pervasive and often leads to training collapse. We argue that this instability stems from inefficient exploration in multi-turn settings, where policies continue to generate low-information actions that neither reduce uncertainty nor advance task progress. To address this issue, we propose Token- and Turn-level Policy Optimization (T$^2$PO), an uncertainty-aware framework that explicitly controls exploration at fine-grained levels. At the token level, T$^2$PO monitors uncertainty dynamics and triggers a thinking intervention once the marginal uncertainty change falls below a threshold. At the turn level, T$^2$PO identifies interactions with negligible exploration progress and dynamically resamples such turns to avoid wasted rollouts. We evaluate T$^2$PO in diverse environments, including WebShop, ALFWorld, and Search QA, demonstrating substantial gains in training stability and performance improvements with better exploration efficiency. Code is available at: https://github.com/WillDreamer/T2PO.

</details>


### [165] [MEMAUDIT: An Exact Package-Oracle Evaluation Protocol for Budgeted Long-Term LLM Memory Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02199)
*Nishant Bhargava,Rodrigo Sobral Barrento*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MEMAUDIT，一种用于评估长期记忆写入的精确协议，通过将记忆选择问题转化为可审计的优化问题，分离记忆表示质量、有效性保持和预算感知选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法通常衡量最终问答准确率，混淆了记忆写入、检索、提示和推理过程，难以定位记忆系统各部分的真实表现。因此需要一种更精确的评估方式来独立评估记忆写入质量。

Method: 提出MEMAUDIT协议，固定经验流、候选记忆表示、存储成本、语义证据单元、未来查询需求和预算，构建一个带约束的记忆覆盖优化问题；采用基于分支定界与MILP认证的方法计算精确最优解。

Result: 在多种测试场景下（包括控制实验、压力测试、人工审核的自然片段以及真实系统导出的记忆），MEMAUDIT能够有效分离记忆表示质量、有效性保持和预算约束下的选择效应，且优于端到端问答评估方法。

Conclusion: MEMAUDIT为长期记忆写入提供了可重复、可认证的评估框架，支持对不同记忆系统在固定存储预算下的表现进行精细比较，并发布了包含生成器、求解器、数据导出和评分工具的完整工具包。

Abstract: Long-term LLM agents must compress streams of past interactions into persistent memory before future queries are known. Existing evaluations usually measure final question-answering accuracy, which entangles memory writing with retrieval, prompting, and reader reasoning. We introduce MEMAUDIT, an exact packageoracle evaluation protocol for budgeted long-term memory writing. A MEMAUDIT package fixes an experience stream, candidate memory representations, storage costs, semantic evidence units, future-query requirements, and a budget, turning write-time memory selection into a finite auditable optimization problem with a certified denominator. We instantiate this protocol with a concave-over-modular semantic coverage objective under storage and one-representation-per-experience constraints, and compute exact package optima using branch-and-bound with MILP certification. Across controlled exact packages, validity-heavy stress tests, human-audited natural support slices, and exported Mem0, A-Mem, and Letta stores, MEMAUDIT separates representation quality, validity-state preservation, and budget-aware selection effects that end-to-end QA cannot localize. The resulting artifact provides reusable package generators, certified solvers, natural package exports, external-system scorers, and cached reproducibility metadata for evaluating what memory writers actually preserve under fixed storage budgets.

</details>


### [166] [CBV: Clean-label Backdoor Attacks on Vision Language Models via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02202)
*Ji Guo,Xiaolong Qin,Cencen Liu,Jielei Wang,Jierun Chen,Wenbo Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型的干净标签后门攻击方法CBV，用于在视觉-语言模型中生成隐蔽的中毒样本，通过分数匹配和GradCAM引导掩码提升攻击的有效性和隐蔽性，在多个数据集和模型上实现了超过80%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM后门攻击依赖于添加视觉触发器和修改文本标签的数据投毒，容易因图像-文本不匹配而被检测到，缺乏隐蔽性。

Method: 提出CBV方法，利用扩散模型通过分数匹配生成自然的中毒样本；在扩散模型的反向生成过程中修改分数以嵌入触发特征，并结合文本信息作为多模态引导；引入GradCAM引导的掩码（GM），限制修改仅发生在语义最重要的区域，增强隐蔽性。

Result: 在MSCOCO和VQA v2数据集上，针对四种代表性VLM进行评估，攻击成功率超过80%，同时保持模型正常功能性能。

Conclusion: CBV是一种高效且隐蔽的干净标签后门攻击方法，能够在不破坏图像-文本一致性的前提下成功攻击VLMs，对现有VLM的安全性构成新的威胁。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved remarkable success in tasks such as image captioning and visual question answering (VQA). However, as their applications become increasingly widespread, recent studies have revealed that VLMs are vulnerable to backdoor attacks. Existing backdoor attacks on VLMs primarily rely on data poisoning by adding visual triggers and modifying text labels, where the induced image-text mismatch makes poisoned samples easy to detect. To address this limitation, we propose the Clean-Label Backdoor Attack on VLMs via Diffusion Models (CBV), which leverages diffusion models to generate natural poisoned examples via score matching. Specifically, CBV modifies the score during the reverse generation process of the diffusion model to guide the generation of poisoned samples that contain triggered image features. To further enhance the effectiveness of the attack, we incorporate the textual information of the triggered images as multimodal guidance during generation. Moreover, to enhance stealthiness, we introduce a GradCAM-guided Mask (GM) that restricts modifications to only the most semantically important regions, rather than the entire image. We evaluate our method on MSCOCO and VQA v2 with four representative VLMs, achieving over 80% ASR while preserving normal functionality.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [LiDAR for Rehabilitation: A Comprehensive Survey of Applications, AI Techniques, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00879)
*Soumia Siyoucef,Najmeddine Dhieb,Hakim Ghazzai,Eleonora Guanziroli,Franco Molteni,Gianluca Setti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了2019至2025年间LiDAR在康复治疗中的应用，涵盖三维人体扫描、步态分析、机器人辅助康复、实时监测与环境感知等方面，重点探讨基于学习的数据处理技术，并通过统计分析揭示研究趋势、空白与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统康复监测技术如摄像头存在隐私问题，可穿戴传感器则可能引起不适并产生误差，因此需要一种更准确、非侵入且可靠的监测手段。LiDAR具备高精度、保护隐私和非接触的优势，具有在康复领域广泛应用的潜力。

Method: 本文采用系统性文献综述方法，收集并分析2019至2025年间关于LiDAR在康复、伤后护理及医院环境中应用的研究，分类讨论其在不同场景下的使用情况，并评估所采用的数据处理技术，特别是基于人工智能的方法。

Result: 研究表明LiDAR在步态分析、身体扫描、机器人辅助康复和活动识别中表现优异；结合AI的处理方法显著提升了数据解析能力；统计分析揭示了当前研究集中于特定应用场景，而在长期临床验证和多模态融合方面仍存在不足。

Conclusion: LiDAR在康复领域展现出巨大潜力，尤其在精准监测和安全交互方面优于传统传感器；本文为该领域提供了首个全面综述，指出了当前技术趋势与挑战，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Rehabilitation aims to help patients with limited mobility regain their physical abilities through targeted movements, exercises, stimulation, and other therapeutic methods. Recent advances in technology have introduced sensor-based systems into rehabilitation and clinical practices, enabling real-time monitoring and providing accurate feedback on movement accuracy. Among these sensors, LiDAR has demonstrated strong potential, offering key advantages over conventional techniques such as camera-based systems, which raise privacy concerns, and wearable sensors, which can be uncomfortable and prone to errors. In this work, we review the applications of LiDAR in rehabilitation, post-injury care, and hospital environments, focusing on studies published between 2019 and 2025. Studies across several areas have been explored: 3D body scanning and gait analysis with standalone LiDAR, LiDAR mounted on robotic systems for rehabilitation, real-time monitoring and environment scanning for safe navigation, and activity and position recognition. We also analyze processing techniques, particularly learning-based approaches, and support the discussion with statistical analysis, highlighting trends, gaps, and future research opportunities. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first comprehensive survey dedicated to LiDAR for rehabilitation applications, providing an overview of current methods, AI-based processing techniques, and open challenges.

</details>


### [168] [Ablation Study of Multimodal Perception, Language Grounding, and Control for Human-Robot Interaction in an Object Detection and Grasping Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00963)
*Zi Tian,Guanting Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过消融研究分析了影响人机交互系统性能的三个关键模块：大语言模型、感知系统和控制器，旨在评估各组件对执行时间和成功率的影响，并确定未来系统改进的关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了明确在多模态人机交互系统中，哪些组件对整体性能影响最大，从而为后续优化提供依据。

Method: 在统一实验协议下，对三种语言模型、五种感知配置和三种控制器进行对比，并在表现最佳的候选者中进行第二阶段的因子分析。

Result: 明确了不同模块对执行时间与任务成功率的影响程度，并识别出最具工程优化潜力的部分。

Conclusion: 该分析有助于指导未来系统设计中的模块选择与优化重点。

Abstract: This manuscript extends our previous multimodal human-robot interaction system by introducing a controlled ablation study of the three modules that most strongly influence end-to-end performance: the large language model used for action extraction, the perception system used for visual grounding, and the controller used for motion execution. The goal is not to redesign the full pipeline, but to isolate the contribution of each component under a common experimental protocol and then evaluate the best combinations end-to-end. We therefore compare three language models, five perception configurations, and three controllers, followed by a second-stage factorial study over the best candidates. The resulting analysis is intended to clarify which choices primarily affect execution time, which primarily affect success rate, and where the largest engineering gains are likely to come from in future revisions of the system.

</details>


### [169] [Value Functions for Temporal Logic: Optimal Policies and Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01051)
*Oswin So,William Sharpless,Sylvia Herbert,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无限时域无折扣设置下，基于Q函数的贪婪策略可能导致任务完成被无限推迟的问题，尤其是在Until规范中。通过将时序逻辑（TL）的价值函数分解为多个子价值函数的图结构，提出了基于状态历史的非马尔可夫策略，并证明其在嵌套Until、Globally及Globally-Until规范下的最优性。同时，展示了Q函数可作为复杂TL规范的安全过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂任务（如时序逻辑规范）中，传统基于最优值函数的贪婪策略可能产生非最优甚至不完备的策略，尤其是在无限时域无折扣情况下，需要建立值最优与策略最优之间的正确对应关系。

Method: 利用近期关于时序逻辑价值函数分解的研究，将整体价值函数拆解为多个构成部分的图结构，基于状态历史构造非马尔可夫策略，并利用Q函数作为安全过滤机制来引导策略执行。

Result: 提出了能够避免任务无限延迟的非马尔可夫策略，并证明了其在嵌套Until、Globally和Globally-Until规范下相对于定量鲁棒性评分的最优性；同时扩展了Q函数作为安全过滤器的应用范围至更复杂的时序逻辑任务。

Conclusion: 仅依赖Q函数的贪婪最大化不足以保证策略最优性，特别是在复杂时序任务中；通过价值函数分解和历史依赖策略可实现真正最优的行为，并提升安全性。

Abstract: While Bellman equations for basic reach, avoid, and reach-avoid problems are well studied, the relationship between value optimality and policy optimality becomes subtle in the undiscounted infinite-horizon setting, particularly for more complicated tasks. Greedily maximizing the Q-function can produce policies that indefinitely defer task completion for reach-avoid problems, or equivalently, Until specifications, even when the value function is optimal. Building upon recent results decomposing the value function for temporal logic (TL) into a graph of constituent value functions, we construct non-Markovian policies based on state history that avoid this pathology and prove their optimality with respect to the quantitative robustness score for nested Until, Globally, and Globally-Until specifications. We further show how the Q function can serve as a safety filter for complex TL specifications, extending prior results beyond simple avoid or reach-avoid tasks.

</details>


### [170] [Online Safety Filter for Deformable Object Manipulation with Horizon Agnostic Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01069)
*Jiaxing Li,Hanjiang Hu,Zhuoyuan Wang,Yorie Nakahira,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于约束的在线安全滤波器，用于可变形物体操作，通过边界控制屏障函数和神经算子实现实时安全约束，显著提高流体操作任务中的安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法通过奖励塑形间接编码安全，无法保证部署时的约束满足，尤其在涉及流体、布料等可变形介质的操作中存在安全隐患。

Method: 结合一种无需固定时间范围的神经算子，学习PDE动力学的边界输入输出映射，并结合边界控制屏障函数，通过轻量级二次规划在任务输出层面实时验证安全性，以最小修改方式过滤任意名义控制策略。

Result: 在FluidLab的流体操作任务中，该方法相比未过滤的基础策略将安全轨迹率提高了最多22%，并减少了进入安全集所需的步数。

Conclusion: 基于约束的安全滤波比奖励塑形更可靠且高效，为可变形物体操作提供了可推广、实时的安全控制框架。

Abstract: Safety critical control of robotic manipulation tasks involving deformable media such as fluids, cloth, and soft objects remains challenging because existing learning based approaches encode safety indirectly through reward shaping, which provides no guarantee of constraint satisfaction at deployment. We present a constraint driven online safety filter for deformable object manipulation that enforces explicit task level safety constraints in real time by minimally modifying any nominal control policy. Our approach combines two key components: a horizon agnostic neural operator that learns the boundary input output mapping of the underlying PDE dynamics and generalizes across variable rollout lengths without retraining, and a boundary control barrier function that certifies safety at the task relevant output level via a lightweight quadratic program. The resulting safety constraint is affine in the boundary input rate, enabling real time online filtering. We evaluate the proposed method on fluid manipulation tasks in FluidLab, where the filter improves safe trajectory rates by up to 22% over unfiltered base policies while also reducing the number of steps required to reach the safe set, demonstrating that constraint driven safety enforcement is both more reliable and more efficient than reward shaping approaches.

</details>


### [171] [Sentinel-VLA: A Metacognitive VLA Model with Active Status Monitoring for Dynamic Reasoning and Error Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01191)
*Wenhao Li,Xiu Su,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Xiaobo Xia,Shan You,Yi Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有元认知能力的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型Sentinel-VLA，通过引入主动监控的“哨兵”模块实现按需推理与错误恢复，结合自进化持续学习算法（SECL）和正交持续适配器（OC-Adapter），在减少计算开销的同时显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型存在推理能力有限、缺乏状态监控和自我纠正困难等问题，难以在复杂或动态环境中稳定执行任务。

Method: 设计了一个名为Sentinel-VLA的元认知模型，包含一个实时监控执行状态的‘哨兵’模块，在必要时触发动态推理或错误恢复；所有训练数据通过自动化管道生成；提出SECL算法以识别能力边界并自动收集扩展数据，结合OC-Adapter防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在涵盖44项任务、超过260万条转换的数据上实现了完全自动化的数据生成；在真实世界实验中，相比最先进模型PI0，任务成功率提升了30%以上。

Conclusion: Sentinel-VLA通过引入元认知机制实现了高效、鲁棒的决策控制，具备强扩展性和实用性，未来将开源全部代码、权重和数据生成流程。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced the field of embodied manipulation by harnessing broad world knowledge and strong generalization. However, current VLA models still face several key challenges, including limited reasoning capability, lack of status monitoring, and difficulty in self-correction. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{Sentinel-VLA}, a metacognitive VLA model equipped with an active ``sentinel'' module to monitor real-time execution status. Only when necessary, such as during initial planning or upon detecting an error, the model triggers a dynamic reasoning or formulate error recovery solutions. This on-demand reasoning mechanism ensures robust decision-making while minimizing computational overhead. Notably, all training data (spanning 44 tasks and over 2.6 million transitions) is automatically generated and annotated through our designed pipeline. We also propose the Self-Evolving Continual Learning (SECL) algorithm, which allows Sentinel-VLA to identify its capability boundaries and automatically collect data for expansion, paired with Orthogonal Continual Adapter (OC-Adapter) to constrain parameter updates to an orthogonal space, thereby preventing catastrophic forgetting. Real-world experiments demonstrate that Sentinel-VLA boosts the task success rate by over 30\% compared to the SOTA model, PI0. We will open-source all the code, weights, and data generation pipeline.

</details>


### [172] [VLA-ATTC: Adaptive Test-Time Compute for VLA Models with Relative Action Critic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01194)
*Wenhao Li,Xiu Su,Dan Niu,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Zhe Qu,Lei Fan,Shan You,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VLA-ATTC的框架，通过自适应测试时计算（TTC）增强视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的决策能力，利用基于不确定性的“认知离合器”和相对动作评论器（RAC）提升复杂任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型依赖快速直觉式决策，缺乏深思熟虑，在面对复杂或模糊情境时容易产生次优或灾难性行为，因此需要引入可自适应的推理机制以提升鲁棒性和性能。

Method: 提出VLA-ATTC框架：1）使用基于不确定性的‘认知离合器’动态判断是否进入TTC深思阶段；2）在TTC阶段通过新设计的相对动作评论器（RAC）对候选动作进行成对比较以选择最优动作；3）采用高效采样策略降低计算开销，并构建自动化数据流水线生成偏好数据对。

Result: 在LIBERO-LONG基准上，VLA-ATTC将当前最优模型PI0.5的失败率降低了50%以上。

Conclusion: VLA-ATTC通过引入自适应的深思机制，显著提升了VLA模型在复杂操作任务中的决策质量与鲁棒性，且具备实际可行性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities and generalization in embodied manipulation. However, their decision-making relies on a fast, instinctive process that lacks deliberation. This strategy often leads to suboptimal or catastrophic actions when facing complex or ambiguous scenarios that require greater consideration. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{VLA-ATTC}, a framework that endows VLA models with adaptive test-time compute (TTC). VLA-ATTC employs an uncertainty-based ``cognitive clutch'' to dynamically transition from reflexive execution to a TTC deliberation phase when necessary. During TTC phase, a novel \textbf{Relative Action Critic} (RAC) model identifies the optimal action from generated candidates via pairwise comparisons. This relative mechanism replaces unstable absolute value estimation, significantly simplifying the learning objective. Furthermore, we introduce an efficient sampling strategy to amortize computational costs and an automated data pipeline that curates preference pairs without manual annotation. On the LIBERO-LONG benchmark, VLA-ATTC reduces the failure rate of the SOTA model PI0.5 by over 50\%. We will open-source all the code and weights.

</details>


### [173] [TAIL-Safe: Task-Agnostic Safety Monitoring for Imitation Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01195)
*Riad Ahmed,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TAIL-Safe的新方法，用于识别模仿学习策略中的安全执行区域，并通过Q函数和恢复机制提升策略在运行时的鲁棒性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习策略虽然性能优秀，但对初始条件敏感且存在累积误差，导致在分布内甚至都会失败，难以安全部署。因此需要一种机制来判断策略是否能在当前状态下安全执行任务。

Method: 提出一个Lipschitz连续的Q值函数，基于可见性、可识别性和可抓取性三个短期无任务依赖标准，评估状态-动作对的长期安全得分；其零超水平集构成经验控制不变集；当策略动作超出该集合时，采用受Nagumo定理启发的梯度上升恢复机制将其拉回安全区域；并通过高保真数字孪生环境（使用高斯溅射构建）系统收集失败数据以训练Q函数。

Result: 在Franka Emika机器人上的实验表明，原本因运行时扰动而失败的流匹配策略，在TAIL-Safe引导下实现了稳定的任务成功率。

Conclusion: TAIL-Safe为模仿学习策略提供了可验证的安全保障机制，显著提升了其在真实场景中的鲁棒性和适用性，推动了IL策略向实际应用的落地。

Abstract: Recent imitation learning (IL) algorithms such as flow-matching and diffusion policies demonstrate remarkable performance in learning complex manipulation tasks. However, these policies often fail even when operating within their training distribution due to extreme sensitivity to initial conditions and irreducible approximation errors that lead to compounding drift. This makes it unsafe to deploy IL policies in the field where out-of-distribution scenarios are prevalent. A prerequisite for safe deployment is enabling the policy to determine whether it can execute a task the way it was learned from demonstrations. This paper presents TAIL-Safe, a principled approach to identify, for a trained IL policy, a safe set from where the policy empirically succeeds in completing the learned task. We propose a Lipschitz-continuous Q-value function that maps state-action pairs to a long-term safety score based on three short-term task-agnostic criteria: visibility, recognizability, and graspability. The zero-superlevel set of this function characterizes an empirical control invariant set over state-action pairs. When the nominal policy proposes an action outside this set, we apply a recovery mechanism inspired by Nagumo's theorem that uses gradient ascent to the Q-function to steer the policy back to safety. To learn this Q-function, we construct a high-fidelity digital twin using Gaussian Splatting that enables systematic collection of failure data without risk to physical hardware. Experiments with a Franka Emika robot demonstrate that flow-matching policies, which fail under run-time perturbations, achieve consistent task success when guided by the proposed TAIL-Safe.

</details>


### [174] [To Do or Not to Do: Ensuring the Safety of Visuomotor Policies Learned from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01201)
*Riad Ahmed,Moniruzzaman Akash,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“执行保证”的策略无关安全度量，用于在模仿学习中确保视觉运动策略的最大任务成功率，并通过视图合成和Nagumo条件识别状态空间中的安全区域，实验证明该方法能在仿真和真实环境中提升策略的安全性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习（IL）研究长期以来以任务成功为主要评价指标，但在实际场外机器人应用中，安全性同样至关重要。然而，IL领域缺乏统一的安全定义，且现有理论难以应用于实际场景，因此亟需一种兼顾安全与性能的分析框架。

Method: 提出“执行保证”这一安全度量，结合视图合成技术识别状态空间中的安全区域，并利用Nagumo的次切向性条件证明在该区域内策略可保持任务成功率；同时设计恢复策略以增强整体性能。

Result: 在Franka机器人上的仿真与实物实验表明，所提方法能有效保证多种IL策略在特定区域内的最大任务成功率，并通过恢复策略提升整体表现，缓解了安全与性能之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文为模仿学习中的策略安全提供了可操作的理论框架，通过执行保证和恢复机制实现了安全性与性能的协同优化，推动了IL在实地机器人应用中的安全部署。

Abstract: Task success has historically been the primary measure of policy performance in imitation learning (IL) research. This characteristics strictly limits the ubiquitous applications of IL algorithms in field robotics where safety assurance, in addition to task-success, is of paramount importance. It is often desirable for an IL-powered robot in the field not to roll out a policy, and hence score a poor performance, if the safety is not guaranteed. Although this trade-off between safety and performance is well investigated in classical control literature, policy safety is a heavily underexplored domain in IL research. There is no universal definition of safety in IL. To make things worst, many existing theoretical works on safety is notoriously difficult to extend to IL-powered robots in the field. This paper offers important insights on the safety and performance of IL policies. We propose execution guarantee, a policy-agnostic safety measure that guarantees the maximum task success for a visuomotor IL policy, despite minor run-time changes, from within a specific region in the state space. We leverage recent advances in view synthesis to identify such regions in the state space for an IL policy and explore a fundamental result on set invariance - namely, Nagumo's sub-tangentiality condition - to prove and operationalize execution guarantee from inside that region. Experiments with a Franka robot, both in simulation and real world, demonstrate how the proposed safety analysis allows various IL policies to achieve maximum task success with guarantee. We also demonstrate some interesting results on how a recovery policy - a by-product of the proposed safety analysis - can help to increase the policy performance and thereby mitigating the safety-performance tradeoff in IL.

</details>


### [175] [Dynamics Aware Quadrupedal Locomotion via Intrinsic Dynamics Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01227)
*Aman Arora,Nalini Ratha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的四足运动训练框架，通过联合训练控制策略和内在动力学头来提升运动效率和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现高效稳定的四足运动，需要理解和估计其底层物理动力学。

Method: 在仿真中同时训练一个学习状态到扭矩动力学的内在动力学头和控制策略，并利用该头定义动力学奖励以促使策略趋向更可预测的动力行为。此外，还提供了通过调整ID头训练系数来调节学习到的动力学的方法。

Result: 模拟实验表明该方法能在多种标准四足运动奖励下收敛到更优解，产生更高效平滑的策略；真实机器人实验验证了这些改进的sim-to-real迁移效果，显著提高了扭矩效率（16.8%）、动作速率（18.6%）和机械功率（12.8%），并改善了安全扭矩占用率（6.4%）。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效提升四足机器人运动的效率、平稳性和安全性，具有良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Quadrupedal locomotion plays a critical role in enabling agile, versatile movement across complex terrains. Understanding and estimating the underlying physical dynamics are essential for achieving efficient and stable quadrupedal locomotion. We propose a novel training framework for quadrupedal locomotion that enables the Control Policy to understand and reason about physical dynamics. In simulation, we concurrently train an Intrinsic Dynamics (ID) Head that learns state-to-torque dynamics alongside the Control Policy, and we define a dynamics reward enabled by the ID Head that encourages the Policy toward more predictable dynamical behavior. We also provide a mechanism to tune the learned dynamics in the resulting Policy by controlling the training coefficients of the ID Head. Our simulation experiments show that this mechanism drives convergence to better optima across a wide range of standard quadrupedal locomotion rewards, yielding more efficient and smoother policies. Our real-robot experiments demonstrate sim-to-real transfer of these improvements, with significant gains in torque efficiency (16.8%), action rate (18.6%), and mechanical power (12.8%), while improving safe torque occupancy by 6.4%.

</details>


### [176] [A Principled Approach for Creating High-fidelity Synthetic Demonstrations for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01232)
*Moniruzzaman Akash,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D高斯点阵（3DGS）和动态运动原语（DMPs）的示范合成框架，能够在保持专家轨迹结构的同时生成多样化的任务示范，并通过解析避障机制实现安全运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3DGS的合成方法在生成新动作时偏离专家轨迹，导致对运动形态敏感的任务中表现不佳，尤其影响接触密集型和形状敏感的操作任务中的策略学习。因此需要将专家轨迹作为强先验来保留其时空结构。

Method: 利用动态运动原语（DMPs）建模专家轨迹，并将其重定向到新的目标、物体配置和视角；结合3DGS重建场景中的连续密度场，提出一种解析的障碍感知DMP公式以实现碰撞避免。

Result: 在Spot移动机械臂上的三个操作任务中验证，相比基于采样规划和轨迹优化的方法，本方法生成的轨迹偏差更小、碰撞率更低，在训练扩散式视觉运动策略时任务成功率更高。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效保留专家运动结构，生成多样化且安全的示范数据，统一了逼真渲染与几何推理，提升了下游策略学习效果。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled visually realistic demonstration generation from a single expert trajectory and a short multi-view scan. However, existing 3DGS-based synthesis pipelines typically generate new motions using sampling-based planners or trajectory optimization, which often deviate substantially from the expert's demonstrated path. While such deviations may be acceptable for tasks insensitive to motion shape, they discard subtle spatial and temporal structure that is critical for contact-rich and shape-sensitive manipulation, causing increased demonstration diversity to harm downstream policy learning. We argue that demonstration synthesis should treat the expert trajectory as a strong prior. Building on this principle, we propose a framework that synthesizes diverse task demonstrations while explicitly preserving expert motion structure. We model the expert trajectory using Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) and retarget it to new goals, object configurations, and viewpoints within a reconstructed 3DGS scene, yielding phase-consistent, shape-preserving motion by construction. To safely realize this expert-preserving diversity in cluttered scenes, we introduce an analytic obstacle-aware DMP formulation that operates directly on the continuous density field induced by the 3DGS representation. This enables collision avoidance while minimally perturbing the nominal expert motion, unifying photorealistic rendering and geometric reasoning without additional scene representations. We evaluate our approach on a Spot mobile manipulator across three manipulation tasks with increasing sensitivity to trajectory fidelity. Compared to planner- and optimization-based synthesis, our method produces trajectories with lower deviation and collision rates and yields higher task success when training diffusion-based visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [177] [Bi-Level Reinforcement Learning Control for an Underactuated Blimp via Center-of-Mass Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01289)
*Xiaorui Wang,Hongwu Wang,Yue Fan,Hao Cheng,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双层强化学习框架，用于具有质心重构的欠驱动飞艇的目标跟踪控制，通过解耦任务级质心规划与推力控制，在仿真和真实环境中均实现了优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提高飞艇的能量效率和有效载荷能力，采用仅含两个推进器和可移动滑块的紧凑型欠驱动设计，但由此带来的强非线性耦合和欠驱动特性对控制提出了挑战，需开发新型控制策略。

Method: 提出一种双层强化学习框架：外层策略根据目标预先规划最优质心配置，内层策略生成推力指令以跟踪直线参考轨迹；并采用两阶段学习策略以保证训练稳定性，辅以双层过程的收敛性分析。

Result: 在27个目标的测试集中，通过大量仿真和真实实验验证，所提方法相比固定质心基线和PID控制器展现出更高的跟踪精度、更强的鲁棒性，并实现了可靠的仿真到真实迁移。

Conclusion: 双层强化学习框架能有效应对欠驱动飞艇中质心动态与运动控制的强耦合问题，通过硬件高效的结构实现高性能目标跟踪，验证了其在实际系统中的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: This paper investigates goal-directed tracking control of underactuated blimps with center-of-mass (CoM) reconfiguration. Unlike conventional overactuated blimp designs that rely on redundant actuation for simplified control, this paper focuses on a compact architecture consisting of two thrusters and a movable internal slider, aiming to improve energy efficiency and payload capacity. This hardware-efficient configuration introduces significant underactuation and strong nonlinear coupling between CoM dynamics and vehicle motion. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a bi-level reinforcement learning framework that explicitly decouples task-level CoM planning from continuous thrust control. The outer policy determines a target-dependent CoM configuration prior to flight, while the inner policy generates thrust commands to track straight-line references. To ensure stable learning, this paper introduces a two-stage learning strategy, supported by a convergence analysis of the resulting bi-level process. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments on a 27-goal evaluation set demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms fixed-CoM baselines and PID-based controllers, achieving higher tracking accuracy, enhanced robustness, and reliable sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [178] [Terrain Perception for Agricultural UAVs in Complex Farmland via Rotating mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01340)
*Zhihao Zhan,Le Tao,Shaobin Li,Chenxin Fang,Xingrui Yang,Liang Li,Rui Fan,Yuhang Ming*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种低成本旋转毫米波雷达系统，用于农业无人机在复杂农田环境中的地形感知，通过机械旋转设计和专为稀疏噪声雷达数据定制的地形重建流程，显著提升了地形覆盖和估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机搭载的毫米波雷达系统视野固定，且地形提取方法依赖密集LiDAR数据，导致在复杂农田环境中地形估计不完整、不可靠。

Method: 引入机械旋转传感设计以扩大空间覆盖范围，并开发了姿态一致的地形重建流程，适用于稀疏、噪声大且部分可观测的雷达数据。

Result: 在真实农业无人机平台上部署并进行了大量实地实验，结果表明该系统将地面分割F1分数提升至94.42，优于次优方法的90.48。

Conclusion: 所提框架显著提高了农业无人机在复杂环境下的地形感知能力，实现了更鲁棒的地形跟随飞行。

Abstract: Accurate terrain perception is essential for terrain-following flight of agricultural unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), yet remains challenging in real-world farmland due to occlusions, complex terrain geometry, and environmental disturbances. Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar is a promising sensing modality for this task due to its robustness to adverse conditions; however, existing UAV-mounted radar systems rely on fixed field of view (FoV) and terrain extraction methods designed for dense LiDAR data, leading to incomplete and unreliable terrain estimation. To address these limitations, we present a low-cost rotating mmWave radar-enabled terrain perception framework for agricultural UAVs operating in complex farmland environments. Specifically, a mechanically rotating sensing design is introduced to enlarge spatial coverage and improve terrain observability beyond the limitations of fixed-view radar under dynamic low-altitude flight. Building upon this sensing capability, we further design a pose-consistent terrain reconstruction pipeline tailored for sparse, noisy, and partially observable radar data, enabling reliable ground extraction and continuous terrain surface estimation in challenging agricultural scenarios. The complete system is deployed on a real agricultural UAV platform and comprehensively evaluated through extensive field experiments. Experimental results demonstrate improved terrain coverage and estimation accuracy, achieving an F1 score of 94.42 for ground segmentation, while the closest rival only achieves 90.48. Thus, leading to more robust terrain following flight.

</details>


### [179] [Assistance Without Interruption: A Benchmark and LLM-based Framework for Non-Intrusive Human-Robot Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01368)
*Yuedi Zhang,Shuanghao Bai,Wanqi Zhou,Haoran Zhang,Qi Zhang,Zhirong Luan,Badong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非侵入式辅助的新型人机交互范式，通过构建仿真基准NIABench和混合架构模型，实现机器人在不打断人类计划的前提下主动提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人机交互常依赖显式指令或干预，可能打断人类原有任务流程；本文旨在建立一种以人类计划为主、机器人辅助为辅的非侵入式协作模式。

Method: 提出一个融合大语言模型与评分模型的混合架构：评分模型先通过语义检索筛选候选动作，再由排序器评估人-机动作对，建模时序与跨步骤依赖关系。

Result: 在NIABench仿真基准和真实场景中验证了方法的有效性，能够实现减少人类负担且不影响任务效率的主动非侵入式辅助。

Conclusion: 非侵入式辅助是一种可行且重要的人机协作新方向，所提架构能有效支持该范式下的决策与协调。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) has long studied how agents and people coordinate to achieve shared goals. In this work, we formalize and benchmark the non-intrusive assistance as an independent paradigm of HRI, where a robot proactively supports a human's ongoing multi-step activities while strictly avoiding interruptions. Unlike conventional HRI tasks that rely on direct commands, explicit negotiation, or proactive interventions based on user habits and history, our task treats the human's plan as the primary process and formulates assistance as a joint decision over when to act and what to do. To systematically evaluate this problem, we establish a simulation benchmark, NIABench, along with new metrics tailored to the non-intrusive assistance task. We further propose a hybrid architecture that integrates an LLM with a scoring model. The scoring model first applies semantic retrieval to prune large candidate action sets, and then a ranker evaluates human-step and robot-action pairs, enabling reasoning over timing and cross-step dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on both NIABench and real-world scenarios demonstrate that our method achieves proactive, non-intrusive assistance that reduces human effort while preserving task effectiveness.

</details>


### [180] [SixthSense: Task-Agnostic Proprioception-Only Whole-Body Wrench Estimation for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01427)
*Xingzhou Chen,Xiayan Xu,Yan Ning,Jiyu Yu,Yizheng Zhang,Siyi Qian,Lingzhu Xiang,Jiahao Chen,Yuquan Wang,Haodong Zhang,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SixthSense是一种无需外部传感器、仅依靠本体感知和IMU数据即可推断全身接触时机、位置和外力矩的通用方法，提升了人形机器人在实际环境中进行力交互的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人形机器人缺乏可靠的接触感知能力，难以执行需要力交互的实际任务，且现有方法依赖理想假设和难以获取的测量数据。

Method: 提出SixthSense方法，利用条件流匹配对本体感知历史进行标记化处理，估计时空稀疏的接触事件流，从而从本体感知和IMU数据中推断全身接触信息。

Result: 在站立、行走和全身运动跟踪等多种行为中实验表明，SixthSense在碰撞检测、人机物理交互和力反馈遥操作等应用中表现出前所未有的性能。

Conclusion: SixthSense作为一种即插即用的感知模块，有效解决了人形机器人在浮动基座和不确定接触下的外力估计难题，推动了其在真实物理环境中的实用化进程。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are entering our physical world at scale, yet as oversized toys--good at singing and dancing, but short on force-interaction capabilities for practical tasks. Bridging this gap necessitates prioritizing reliable contact perception as a fundamental requirement. Estimating external wrenches in humanoids is complicated by floating-base dynamics and indeterminate contact locations. Existing analytical frameworks require idealistic assumptions and hard-to-obtain measurements, which are often unavailable in practice. To bridge this gap, we propose SixthSense, a task-agnostic approach that infers whole-body contact timing, location, and wrenches from proprioception and IMU data alone. To capture the multi-modal dynamics between unstructured contact inputs and the uncertain motion outputs, we employ conditional flow matching to tokenize proprioceptive histories and estimate a spatiotemporally sparse contact-event flow. SixthSense serves as a plug-and-play perception module for applications including collision detection, physical human-robot interaction, and force-feedback teleoperation. Experiments across standing, walking, and whole-body motion-tracking policies showcased unprecedented performance in diverse behaviors.

</details>


### [181] [Evidence-Based Landing Site Selection and Vison-Based Landing for UAVs in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01432)
*Sina Sajjadi,Jacopo Panerati,Sina Soleymanpour,Varunkumar Mehta,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi,Iraj Mantegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于证据的自主无人机着陆概率框架，通过分离决策与执行来提高在感知噪声和复杂环境下的着陆安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机在杂乱或非结构化环境中因传感器不确定性和平台扰动导致感知噪声而面临的自主着陆安全挑战。

Method: 采用基于视觉平坦度、坡度和障碍物线索的帧间似然递归累积，构建时序一致的置信图，并结合最小着陆半径的硬几何约束，通过约束最大后验估计选择最终着陆点，利用ORB特征跟踪和基于图像的视觉伺服实现精确对准和降落。

Result: 在真实实验室实验和Nvidia Isaac Sim高保真仿真中验证了该方法的有效性，表现出跨域一致、谨慎且稳定的着陆行为。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效提升无人机在感知噪声和复杂环境下的自主着陆安全性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous landing in cluttered or unstructured environments remains a safety-critical challenge for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), particularly under noisy perception caused by sensor uncertainty and platform-induced disturbances such as vibration. This paper presents an evidence-based probabilistic framework for autonomous UAV landing that explicitly separates decision-making under uncertainty from execution via visual servoing. Landing safety is modeled as a latent variable and inferred through recursive accumulation of frame-wise visual likelihoods derived from flatness, slope, and obstacle cues, yielding a temporally consistent belief map that is robust to transient perception errors. Physical feasibility is enforced through a hard geometric constraint based on the minimum required landing radius of the UAV, ensuring that undersized but visually appealing regions are rejected. The final landing site is selected using constrained maximum a posteriori estimation. Once selected, the UAV locks onto the target region using ORB feature tracking and performs precise alignment and descent via image-based visual servoing (IBVS). The proposed approach is validated through both real-world laboratory experiments and high-fidelity simulations in Nvidia Isaac Sim, demonstrating consistent, cautious, and stable landing behavior across domains.

</details>


### [182] [High-Speed, Scalable Sensor Readout for Dexterous Robotic Hands via Shift-Register Multiplexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01434)
*Jaehoon Kim,Lazaros Christoforidis,Michalis Papadakis,Victor Kartsch,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于串行输入并行输出（SIPO）移位寄存器原理的可扩展模拟传感器读出架构，支持高通道数、低布线复杂度和高速采样，验证于一个集成16个关节传感器和1个四通道触觉传感器的灵巧手系统，实现了1 kHz全扫描速率下的稳定传感，并展示了优越的感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人手的传感器读出架构在传感器数量、布线复杂度和采样带宽之间存在权衡，难以满足灵巧操作对高密度、多模态、高速传感的需求。

Method: 提出一种基于SIPO移位寄存器的模拟传感器读出架构，仅用三条信号线实现模块间连接，支持异构模拟传感器的灵活集成与可扩展扩展，并在腱驱动机器人手上集成16个关节传感器模块和1个四通道触觉传感器模块进行实验验证。

Result: 实现了20个传感器通道在1 kHz全扫描速率下的数据采集，稳定工作可达1.5 kHz；关节传感器最大绝对百分比误差为0.446%，角度估计误差低于1度；触觉感知中LSTM模型力估计RMSE为0.125 N，五类接触位置分类准确率达93.4%；系统实验证明关节传感器反馈优于电机端估计，触觉传感器可实现快速响应的接触力气估计。

Conclusion: 该读出架构为实现面向灵巧操作的全传感化机器人手提供了一条实用的技术路径，兼顾了高采样率、低布线复杂度与可扩展性，且未显著降低传感性能。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands require high-speed multimodal sensing across many degrees of freedom, yet existing readout architectures often impose trade-offs between sensor count, wiring complexity, and sampling bandwidth. This paper presents a scalable analog sensor readout architecture based on a serial-in parallel-out (SIPO) shift-register principle. The proposed architecture supports versatile integration of heterogeneous analog-output sensors, scalable expansion using only three signal lines between sensor modules, and fast, configurable sampling. We validate the approach on a tendon-driven robotic hand integrating 16 joint sensor modules and one four-channel tactile sensor module, enabling acquisition of 20 sensor channels at a full-scan rate of 1 kHz, with stable operation up to 1.5 kHz. Joint sensor characterization showed a maximum slope absolute percentage error (APE) of 0.446% and sub-degree estimation error, indicating that the proposed readout system does not significantly degrade sensing performance. For tactile sensing, LSTM-based models achieved an RMSE of 0.125 N for force estimation and 93.4% accuracy for five-class contact-location classification, and were deployed for real-time inference at 1 kHz. System-level experiments showed that the joint sensors provide more accurate feedback than motor-based estimation during interaction, while the tactile sensor enables responsive force estimation in contact. The proposed architecture offers a practical path toward fully sensorized robotic hands for dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [183] [LLM-Foraging: Large Language Models for Decentralized Swarm Robot Foraging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01461)
*Peihan Li,Joanna Gutierrez,Fabian Hernandez,Qi Lu,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Foraging，一种结合大语言模型的去中心化群体觅食控制方法，无需训练即可跨不同配置迁移，并在多种环境下优于传统遗传算法优化的CPFA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群体觅食算法依赖离线参数优化，对特定配置过拟合，环境变化时性能下降且重训练成本高，缺乏泛化能力。

Method: 在CPFA状态机的三个决策点（存贮后、中心区到达、搜索饥饿）引入大语言模型作为战术决策器，每个机器人基于局部可观测状态独立查询本地LLM客户端，原有运动与感知模块执行动作。

Result: 在Gazebo中使用TurtleBot3进行测试，覆盖36种配置（4-10台机器人、6x6至10x10米场地、三种资源分布），LLM-Foraging在资源收集量和表现一致性上均优于GA调优的CPFA基线。

Conclusion: LLM可作为通用决策策略用于群体机器人控制，实现免训练部署与跨配置迁移，提升系统适应性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Swarm foraging algorithms, such as the central-place foraging algorithm (CPFA), typically rely on offline parameter optimization using genetic algorithms (GA) or reinforcement learning, yielding policies tightly coupled to a specific combination of team size, arena size, and resource distribution. When deployment conditions change, performance degrades, and retraining is computationally expensive. We propose LLM-Foraging, a decentralized swarm controller that augments the CPFA state machine with a large language model (LLM) tactical decision-maker at three structured decision points, namely post-deposit, central-zone arrival, and search starvation. Each robot runs its own LLM client and queries it using only locally observable state, while the existing CPFA motion and sensing stack executes the selected action. Because the LLM serves as a general decision policy rather than parameters fitted to a single configuration, the controller is training-free at deployment and transfers across configurations without re-optimization. We evaluate LLM-Foraging in Gazebo with TurtleBot3 robots across 36 configurations spanning team sizes of 4 to 10 robots, arena sizes from 6x6 to 10x10 meters, and three resource distributions (clustered, powerlaw, random). LLM-Foraging collects more resources than the GA-tuned CPFA baseline across the evaluated configurations and is more consistent, a property that the GA's single-configuration tuning does not transfer.

</details>


### [184] [Cut-In Gap Acceptance Toward Autonomous vs. Human-Driven Vehicles: Evidence from the Waymo Open Motion Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01485)
*Abdulaziz Alhuraish,Yuhang Wang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现人类驾驶员在变道至自动驾驶车辆（AV）前方时接受的纵向间距更短，且切入速度更高，表明人类对AV的行为存在系统性差异，需针对AV进行运动规划和交通模拟模型的校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于自动驾驶车辆通常遵循保守、基于规则的驾驶策略，周围驾驶员可能学会预判其行为，从而导致在变道时对其采取不同的间距策略。本研究旨在验证人类驾驶员是否会以更短的间距切入AV前方。

Method: 使用Waymo开放运动数据集（WOMD），提取25,906个真实高速公路场景中的706次人类驾驶车辆（HDV）向AV切入和3,172次向HDV切入事件，通过八准则变道检测器分析接受间距、速度等参数，并进行统计检验与速度匹配重采样分析。

Result: AV前方的中位接受间距为7.58米，显著低于HDV的9.57米（p=5.76×10⁻⁸，d=-0.224）；切入速度平均高出37%（51.7 vs 37.7 km/h，d=0.502）；68.0%的AV切入发生在10米以内，显著高于HDV的51.8%（χ²=60.5，p<10⁻¹³）。结果在速度匹配后依然成立。

Conclusion: 人类驾驶员对AV表现出更激进的切入行为，揭示了人车交互中的系统性安全相关不对称性，建议在AV的运动规划安全边界和交通仿真建模中进行专门校准。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) are widely known to follow conservative, rule-based motion policies that surrounding drivers can learn to anticipate. A direct consequence is that human drivers may accept shorter longitudinal gaps when cutting in front of an AV than when targeting another human-driven vehicle (HDV). We test this hypothesis using the Waymo Open Motion Dataset (WOMD), which provides 25,906 real-world highway scenarios at 10 hertz. An eight-criterion lane-change detector extracts 706 HDV-to-AV and 3,172 HDV-to-HDV cut-in events from the same traffic environment. The median accepted gap in front of the Waymo AV is 7.58 meters versus 9.57 meters for HDV targets, a 1.99 meter reduction that is statistically significant (p equals 5.76 times 10 to the negative eighth power, d equals negative 0.224) and persists under speed-matched resampling. Cut-in speeds toward the AV are 37 percent higher (51.7 versus 37.7 kilometers per hour, d equals 0.502), and 68.0 percent of AV-targeted cut-ins occur below the 10 meter gap boundary versus 51.8 percent of HDV-targeted events (chi-squared equals 60.5, p is less than 10 to the negative thirteenth power). These results reveal a systematic and safety-relevant asymmetry in human gap-acceptance behavior that warrants AV-specific calibration of both motion-planning safety envelopes and traffic simulation models.

</details>


### [185] [Distributed Algorithm with Emergent Area Partitioning and Base Station's Situation Awareness for Multi-Robot Patrolling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01501)
*Kazuho Kobayashi,Shohei Kobayashi,Seiya Ueno,Takehiro Higuchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LR-PT的新型多机器人巡逻算法，通过局部反应与区域划分机制提升巡逻效率和基站的情境感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高多机器人巡逻系统的效率及操作员在基站的情境感知能力，以更好预测机器人行为、支持决策并实现应急干预。

Method: 提出LR-PT算法，机器人基于本地信息独立选择巡逻目标，结合巡逻需求与任务汇报紧迫性构建统一效用函数，并自主形成区域划分。

Result: 仿真结果表明，LR-PT在频繁覆盖关注区域、增强基站情境感知、应对通信限制和机器人故障方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LR-PT算法有效提升了多机器人巡逻性能，兼具鲁棒性与全局覆盖能力，适用于实际应用环境。

Abstract: Patrolling with multiple robots offers efficient surveillance to detect and manage undesired situations. This necessitates improved patrol efficiency and operator situation awareness at base stations. Enhanced situation awareness enables operators to predict robots' behaviors, support recognition and decision-making, and execute emergency interventions. This study presents the Local Reactive and Partition (LR-PT) algorithm, a novel multi-robot patrolling approach. In simulations, LR-PT outperformed existing methods by ensuring frequent patrols of all locations of interest and enhancing the situation awareness of the base station. Robots independently select patrol targets based on locally available information, integrating patrol needs and the urgency of reporting mission progress to the base station into a unified utility function. This locality also contributes to robustness against communication constraints and robot failures, as demonstrated in this research. The algorithm further autonomously emerged the area partition, which can avoid falling into local optima and realize the comprehensive patrol over the whole mission area. The simulation results demonstrated the superior performance of LR-PT for multi-robot patrolling, utilizing the advantages of swarm robotics and addressing real-world operational challenges.

</details>


### [186] [Dynamics Distillation for Efficient and Transferable Control Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01516)
*Xunjiang Gu,Kashyap Chitta,Mahsa Golchoubian,Vladimir Suplin,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sim2Sim2Sim提出了一种框架，通过将高保真模拟器的动力学蒸馏为可高度并行的学习模型，实现高效且可靠的强化学习策略训练与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶仿真环境难以同时满足物理真实性和计算可扩展性，限制了鲁棒控制策略的学习。

Method: 提出Sim2Sim2Sim框架，利用知识蒸馏将高保真模拟器的动力学建模为可大规模并行的 learned dynamics 模型，并在此环境中训练强化学习策略，再迁回原高保真模拟器验证。

Result: 在挑战性动力学条件下实现了更高效的政策优化和可靠转移；发现仅用预测精度不足以评估学习动力学模型作为强化学习训练环境的有效性。

Conclusion: 学习动力学模型不仅需准确预测状态转移，更应能支持高质量策略的训练，其有效性应以所生成策略的质量来综合评估。

Abstract: Robust control policy learning for autonomous driving requires training environments to be both physically realistic and computationally scalable, properties that existing simulators provide only in isolation. We introduce Sim2Sim2Sim, a framework that bridges high-fidelity vehicle simulation and scalable reinforcement learning by distilling simulator dynamics into a highly parallelizable learned dynamics model. By training control policies purely within this distilled environment and deploying them back into the high-fidelity source simulator, we demonstrate more efficient policy optimization and reliable transfer under challenging dynamics. We further show that predictive accuracy alone does not fully characterize a learned dynamics model's suitability as a reinforcement learning training environment, which should also be assessed by the quality of the policies it enables.

</details>


### [187] [VOFA: Visual Object Goal Pushing with Force-Adaptive Control for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01518)
*Zichao Hu,Zifan Xu,Dongsik Chang,He Yin,Linh Tran,Roberto Martín-Martín,Peter Stone,Jingyu Qiao,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VOFA是一种基于视觉目标条件的人形机器人操作系统，能够在未知物体物理属性和机载感知条件下，实现对任意目标位置的重物推动任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于特权信息或缺乏对目标配置和物体物理特性的鲁棒性，难以在真实环境中有效推动重物。因此需要一种能够基于机载感知、适应未知环境变化的实时闭环控制系统。

Method: 提出VOFA，采用两级分层架构：高层为视觉运动策略，处理噪声感知并生成目标导向指令；底层为力自适应全身控制器，增强对物体质量、摩擦等物理特性变化的鲁棒性。系统通过仿真与真实实验进行验证。

Result: 在仿真中成功率超过90%，真实世界实验中超过80%；成功推动最重达17kg的物体，超过Booster T1人形机器人自重的一半。
